## `Guitar/ChordAnalyzer.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

/// <summary>
/// 游戏四页 24 和弦元数据定义
/// </summary>
public sealed class ChordDefinition
{
    public ChordId Id { get; }
    public string Name { get; }
    public byte Page { get; } // 1..4
    public byte Slot { get; } // 1..6
    public byte RootPitchClass { get; } // 0..11
    public HashSet<byte> PitchClasses { get; }
    public byte? SpecialBassPitchClass { get; } // 用于 Slash Chord (G/B, Am7/G, D/F#, F/A)

    public ChordDefinition(
        ChordId id,
        string name,
        byte page,
        byte slot,
        byte root,
        IEnumerable<byte> tones,
        byte? specialBass = null)
    {
        Id = id;
        Name = name;
        Page = page;
        Slot = slot;
        RootPitchClass = root;
        PitchClasses = new HashSet<byte>(tones);
        SpecialBassPitchClass = specialBass;
    }

    public override string ToString() => $"[P{Page} Slot{Slot}] {Name}";
}

public sealed class ChordAnalyzerOptions
{
    public ChordRecognitionMode Mode { get; set; } = ChordRecognitionMode.Loose;
    public long AttackClusterUs { get; set; } = 80_000;    // 攻击聚类窗口 80ms (适合攻击形态聚类)
    public long AnalysisWindowUs { get; set; } = 450_000;  // 观察分析窗口 450ms
    public double HysteresisMargin { get; set; } = 0.12;   // 迟滞防抖超额阈值
    public double MinHoldScore { get; set; } = 0.25;        // 最低保持阈值
    public long MinChordStabilityUs { get; set; } = 180_000; // 最小和弦稳定区 180ms (150~250ms 防止相近和弦高频抖动)
}

/// <summary>
/// 24 和弦分析器: 支持游戏全部 4 页 24 和弦识别、Slash Chord 低音判定与防抖 (v4.3 升级)
/// </summary>
public static class ChordAnalyzer
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 游戏真实存在的 24 个和弦目录 (4页，每页 6 槽位，完全对齐游戏截图)
    /// </summary>
    public static readonly IReadOnlyList<ChordDefinition> SupportedChords = new ChordDefinition[]
    {
        // ================= Page 1 =================
        new(ChordId.G,        "G",     1, 1, 7,  new byte[] { 7, 11, 2 }),          // Slot 1
        new(ChordId.Am,       "Am",    1, 2, 9,  new byte[] { 9, 0, 4 }),           // Slot 2
        new(ChordId.Bm,       "Bm",    1, 3, 11, new byte[] { 11, 2, 6 }),          // Slot 3
        new(ChordId.C,        "C",     1, 4, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 4, 7 }),           // Slot 4
        new(ChordId.D,        "D",     1, 5, 2,  new byte[] { 2, 6, 9 }),           // Slot 5
        new(ChordId.Em,       "Em",    1, 6, 4,  new byte[] { 4, 7, 11 }),          // Slot 6

        // ================= Page 2 =================
        new(ChordId.Cmaj7,    "Cmaj7", 2, 1, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 4, 7, 11 }),       // Slot 1
        new(ChordId.C7,       "C7",    2, 2, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 4, 7, 10 }),       // Slot 2
        new(ChordId.Cm7,      "Cm7",   2, 3, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 3, 7, 10 }),       // Slot 3
        new(ChordId.G7,       "G7",    2, 4, 7,  new byte[] { 7, 11, 2, 5 }),       // Slot 4
        new(ChordId.C9,       "C9",    2, 5, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 4, 7, 10, 2 }),    // Slot 5
        new(ChordId.Cm9,      "Cm9",   2, 6, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 3, 7, 10, 2 }),    // Slot 6

        // ================= Page 3 =================
        new(ChordId.C5,       "C5",    3, 1, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 7 }),              // Slot 1
        new(ChordId.D5,       "D5",    3, 2, 2,  new byte[] { 2, 9 }),              // Slot 2
        new(ChordId.G5,       "G5",    3, 3, 7,  new byte[] { 7, 2 }),              // Slot 3
        new(ChordId.A5,       "A5",    3, 4, 9,  new byte[] { 9, 4 }),              // Slot 4
        new(ChordId.Cmaj9,    "Cmaj9", 3, 5, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 4, 7, 11, 2 }),    // Slot 5
        new(ChordId.Cadd9,    "Cadd9", 3, 6, 0,  new byte[] { 0, 4, 7, 2 }),        // Slot 6

        // ================= Page 4 =================
        new(ChordId.G_B,      "G/B",   4, 1, 7,  new byte[] { 7, 11, 2 }, specialBass: 11), // Slot 1: B 低音
        new(ChordId.Am7_G,    "Am7/G", 4, 2, 9,  new byte[] { 9, 0, 4, 7 }, specialBass: 7), // Slot 2: G 低音
        new(ChordId.D_Fsharp, "D/F#",  4, 3, 2,  new byte[] { 2, 6, 9 }, specialBass: 6),   // Slot 3: F# 低音
        new(ChordId.F_A,      "F/A",   4, 4, 5,  new byte[] { 5, 9, 0 }, specialBass: 9),   // Slot 4: A 低音
        new(ChordId.A,        "A",     4, 5, 9,  new byte[] { 9, 1, 4 }),           // Slot 5
        new(ChordId.Dm,       "Dm",    4, 6, 2,  new byte[] { 2, 5, 9 })            // Slot 6
    };

    private static readonly Dictionary<ChordId, ChordDefinition> _chordMap =
        SupportedChords.ToDictionary(c => c.Id);

    public static ChordDefinition? GetDefinition(ChordId id) =>
        _chordMap.TryGetValue(id, out var def) ? def : null;

    /// <summary>
    /// 分析 MIDI 音符并生成和弦切换时间线 (兼容旧版输出)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<ChordChangeEvent> Analyze(
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawNotes,
        ChordAnalyzerOptions? options = null)
    {
        var detailed = AnalyzeDetailed(rawNotes, options);
        var events = new List<ChordChangeEvent>();
        ChordId current = ChordId.Unknown;

        foreach (var ev in detailed)
        {
            if (ev.Chord != current && ev.Chord != ChordId.Unknown)
            {
                current = ev.Chord;
                events.Add(new ChordChangeEvent(ev.StartUs, ev.Chord, (float)ev.Confidence, ev.Page, ev.Slot));
            }
        }

        return events;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 分析 MIDI 和声流并生成保留源音符与演奏形态的详细和弦事件列表 (方案 6.2 & 6.3)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<ChordEvent> AnalyzeDetailed(
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawNotes,
        ChordAnalyzerOptions? options = null)
    {
        if (rawNotes == null || rawNotes.Count == 0)
            return new List<ChordEvent>();

        options ??= new ChordAnalyzerOptions();

        // 过滤打击乐通道
        var notes = rawNotes.Where(n => n.Channel != 9).OrderBy(n => n.StartUs).ToList();
        if (notes.Count == 0) return new List<ChordEvent>();

        // 1. 攻击形态聚类
        var attackClusters = ClusterAttacks(notes, options.AttackClusterUs);

        var events = new List<ChordEvent>();
        ChordId currentChord = ChordId.Unknown;
        ChordId consecutiveCandidate = ChordId.Unknown;
        int consecutiveCount = 0;
        long lastSwitchTimeUs = -options.MinChordStabilityUs;

        foreach (var cluster in attackClusters)
        {
            long attackTime = cluster[0].StartUs;
            long winEnd = attackTime + options.AnalysisWindowUs;

            // 统计分析窗口内持续发声的活跃音符 (Active Notes)
            double[] evidence = new double[12];
            double totalEvidence = 0;
            int lowestMidiNote = int.MaxValue;
            byte lowestBassPc = 255;
            var activeNotes = new List<RawMidiNote>();

            foreach (var note in notes)
            {
                long noteEnd = note.StartUs + (note.DurationUs > 0 ? note.DurationUs : 300_000);
                if (note.StartUs >= winEnd || noteEnd <= attackTime) continue;

                long overlap = Math.Min(noteEnd, winEnd) - Math.Max(note.StartUs, attackTime);
                if (overlap <= 0) continue;

                activeNotes.Add(note);
                double weight = (overlap / (double)options.AnalysisWindowUs) * (note.Velocity / 127.0);
                byte pc = (byte)(note.NoteNumber % 12);
                evidence[pc] += weight;
                totalEvidence += weight;

                if (note.NoteNumber < lowestMidiNote)
                {
                    lowestMidiNote = note.NoteNumber;
                    lowestBassPc = pc;
                }
            }

            if (totalEvidence < 0.05) continue;

            // 2. 在全部 24 个和弦模板中评分并选择最优和弦
            var (bestChord, bestScore, currentScore) = ScoreAll24Chords(
                evidence,
                totalEvidence,
                lowestBassPc,
                currentChord,
                options.Mode);

            // 3. 稳定区与迟滞防抖逻辑 (Hysteresis + Stability Window)
            bool shouldSwitch = false;
            long timeSinceLastSwitch = attackTime - lastSwitchTimeUs;

            if (currentChord == ChordId.Unknown)
            {
                if (bestScore >= 0.35) shouldSwitch = true;
            }
            else
            {
                if (bestChord == currentChord)
                {
                    consecutiveCandidate = ChordId.Unknown;
                    consecutiveCount = 0;
                }
                else
                {
                    // 必须满足最小稳定区 (150~250ms) 或者是极显著和弦变更
                    if (timeSinceLastSwitch >= options.MinChordStabilityUs || bestScore >= (currentScore + 0.35))
                    {
                        if (bestScore >= currentScore + options.HysteresisMargin)
                        {
                            shouldSwitch = true;
                        }
                        else if (currentScore < options.MinHoldScore && bestScore >= 0.35)
                        {
                            shouldSwitch = true;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            if (bestChord == consecutiveCandidate)
                            {
                                consecutiveCount++;
                                if (consecutiveCount >= 2 && bestScore >= 0.35) shouldSwitch = true;
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                consecutiveCandidate = bestChord;
                                consecutiveCount = 1;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (shouldSwitch && bestChord != ChordId.Unknown && bestChord != currentChord)
            {
                currentChord = bestChord;
                lastSwitchTimeUs = attackTime;
                consecutiveCandidate = ChordId.Unknown;
                consecutiveCount = 0;
            }

            // 4. 判定攻击演奏形态 (Attack Articulation: Block / Strum / Arpeggio)
            var (articulation, dir) = DetectAttackArticulation(cluster);

            var activeId = (currentChord != ChordId.Unknown) ? currentChord : bestChord;
            var def = GetDefinition(activeId);
            byte page = def?.Page ?? 1;
            byte slot = def?.Slot ?? 1;

            var ev = new ChordEvent
            {
                StartUs = attackTime,
                EndUs = winEnd,
                Chord = activeId,
                Page = page,
                Slot = slot,
                Confidence = Math.Clamp(bestScore, 0.0, 1.0),
                SourceNotes = activeNotes,
                AttackNotes = cluster.ToList(),
                Articulation = articulation,
                Direction = dir
            };

            events.Add(ev);
        }

        return events;
    }

    private static List<List<RawMidiNote>> ClusterAttacks(List<RawMidiNote> notes, long clusterUs)
    {
        var clusters = new List<List<RawMidiNote>>();
        if (notes.Count == 0) return clusters;

        var cur = new List<RawMidiNote> { notes[0] };
        long start = notes[0].StartUs;

        for (int i = 1; i < notes.Count; i++)
        {
            if (notes[i].StartUs - start <= clusterUs)
            {
                cur.Add(notes[i]);
            }
            else
            {
                clusters.Add(cur);
                cur = new List<RawMidiNote> { notes[i] };
                start = notes[i].StartUs;
            }
        }

        if (cur.Count > 0) clusters.Add(cur);
        return clusters;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 24 和弦评分公式 (文档 6.2)
    /// score = 0.58 * chordToneCoverage + 0.30 * expectedToneCompleteness + rootPresenceBonus + bassMatchBonus - extraTonePenalty - missingTonePenalty
    /// </summary>
    public static (ChordId BestChord, double BestScore, double CurrentScore) ScoreAll24Chords(
        double[] evidence,
        double totalEvidence,
        byte lowestBassPc,
        ChordId currentChord,
        ChordRecognitionMode mode)
    {
        ChordId bestChord = ChordId.Unknown;
        double bestScore = double.MinValue;
        double currentChordScore = 0;

        foreach (var def in SupportedChords)
        {
            double toneEvidence = 0;
            double nonToneEvidence = 0;
            int tonesPresent = 0;

            for (byte pc = 0; pc < 12; pc++)
            {
                if (def.PitchClasses.Contains(pc))
                {
                    toneEvidence += evidence[pc];
                    if (evidence[pc] > 0.02) tonesPresent++;
                }
                else
                {
                    nonToneEvidence += evidence[pc];
                }
            }

            double chordToneCoverage = toneEvidence / Math.Max(0.001, totalEvidence);
            double expectedToneCompleteness = tonesPresent / (double)def.PitchClasses.Count;

            // 根音奖励
            double rootBonus = (evidence[def.RootPitchClass] > 0.02) ? 0.12 : 0.0;

            // 特殊低音 (Slash Chord) 与常规低音判定
            double bassBonus = 0;
            if (def.SpecialBassPitchClass.HasValue)
            {
                if (lowestBassPc == def.SpecialBassPitchClass.Value)
                {
                    bassBonus = 0.40; // 斜杠和弦低音精确匹配
                }
                else
                {
                    bassBonus = -0.55; // 低音不符时强烈惩罚，防止普通和弦误判为斜杠和弦
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (lowestBassPc == def.RootPitchClass)
                {
                    bassBonus = 0.18; // 普通和弦根音为低音
                }
            }

            // 非和弦音与缺失音惩罚
            double extraPenalty = (mode == ChordRecognitionMode.Strict) ? 0.45 * (nonToneEvidence / Math.Max(0.001, totalEvidence))
                                                                       : 0.25 * (nonToneEvidence / Math.Max(0.001, totalEvidence));
            double missingPenalty = 0.15 * (1.0 - expectedToneCompleteness);

            double score = 0.58 * chordToneCoverage
                         + 0.30 * expectedToneCompleteness
                         + rootBonus
                         + bassBonus
                         - extraPenalty
                         - missingPenalty;

            // 历史连续性奖励
            if (def.Id == currentChord)
            {
                score += 0.15;
                currentChordScore = score;
            }

            if (score > bestScore)
            {
                bestScore = score;
                bestChord = def.Id;
            }
        }

        return (bestChord, bestScore, currentChordScore);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 获取 24 个和弦各弦的发音音高 (1=最高音弦 E4, 6=最低音弦 E2)
    /// </summary>
    public static byte[] GetChordStringPitches(ChordId chord) => chord switch
    {
        // Page 1
        ChordId.G        => new byte[] { 67, 59, 55, 50, 47, 43 }, // G4, B3, G3, D3, B2, G2
        ChordId.Am       => new byte[] { 64, 60, 57, 52, 45, 40 }, // E4, C4, A3, E3, A2, E2
        ChordId.Bm       => new byte[] { 66, 62, 59, 54, 47, 42 }, // F#4, D4, B3, F#3, B2, F#2
        ChordId.C        => new byte[] { 64, 60, 55, 52, 48, 40 }, // E4, C4, G3, E3, C3, E2
        ChordId.D        => new byte[] { 66, 62, 57, 50, 45, 38 }, // F#4, D4, A3, D3, A2, D2
        ChordId.Em       => new byte[] { 64, 59, 55, 52, 47, 40 }, // E4, B3, G3, E3, B2, E2

        // Page 2
        ChordId.Cmaj7    => new byte[] { 64, 59, 55, 52, 48, 40 }, // E4, B3, G3, E3, C3, E2
        ChordId.C7       => new byte[] { 64, 58, 55, 52, 48, 40 }, // E4, Bb3, G3, E3, C3, E2
        ChordId.Cm7      => new byte[] { 63, 58, 55, 51, 48, 39 }, // Eb4, Bb3, G3, Eb3, C3, Eb2
        ChordId.G7       => new byte[] { 67, 59, 53, 50, 47, 43 }, // G4, B3, F3, D3, B2, G2
        ChordId.C9       => new byte[] { 62, 58, 55, 52, 48, 40 }, // D4, Bb3, G3, E3, C3, E2
        ChordId.Cm9      => new byte[] { 62, 58, 55, 51, 48, 39 }, // D4, Bb3, G3, Eb3, C3, Eb2

        // Page 3
        ChordId.C5       => new byte[] { 67, 60, 55, 48, 43, 36 }, // G4, C4, G3, C3, G2, C2
        ChordId.D5       => new byte[] { 69, 62, 57, 50, 45, 38 }, // A4, D4, A3, D3, A2, D2
        ChordId.G5       => new byte[] { 67, 55, 55, 50, 43, 43 }, // G4, G3, G3, D3, G2, G2
        ChordId.A5       => new byte[] { 69, 57, 57, 52, 45, 45 }, // A4, A3, A3, E3, A2, A2
        ChordId.Cmaj9    => new byte[] { 62, 59, 55, 52, 48, 40 }, // D4, B3, G3, E3, C3, E2
        ChordId.Cadd9    => new byte[] { 62, 60, 55, 52, 48, 40 }, // D4, C4, G3, E3, C3, E2

        // Page 4
        ChordId.G_B      => new byte[] { 67, 59, 55, 50, 47, 47 }, // B2 低音
        ChordId.Am7_G    => new byte[] { 64, 60, 57, 52, 45, 43 }, // G2 低音
        ChordId.D_Fsharp => new byte[] { 66, 62, 57, 50, 45, 42 }, // F#2 低音
        ChordId.F_A      => new byte[] { 65, 60, 57, 53, 45, 45 }, // A2 低音
        ChordId.A        => new byte[] { 64, 61, 57, 52, 45, 40 }, // E4, C#4, A3, E3, A2, E2
        ChordId.Dm       => new byte[] { 65, 62, 57, 50, 45, 38 }, // F4, D4, A3, D3, A2, D2

        _                => new byte[] { 64, 59, 55, 50, 45, 40 }  // 默认空弦 E4, B3, G3, D3, A2, E2
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// 将目标音符智能匹配到当前和弦下的最优琴弦 (1..6)
    /// </summary>
    public static byte MatchNoteToChordString(byte noteNumber, ChordId chord, HashSet<byte>? occupiedStrings = null)
    {
        var pitches = GetChordStringPitches(chord);

        // 1. 精确音高匹配且未被占用
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            byte strNo = (byte)(i + 1);
            if (occupiedStrings != null && occupiedStrings.Contains(strNo)) continue;
            if (pitches[i] == noteNumber) return strNo;
        }

        // 2. 音名 (Pitch Class) 一致，在未占用琴弦中选择绝对音高最接近的
        byte targetPc = (byte)(noteNumber % 12);
        byte bestString = 0;
        int minDistance = int.MaxValue;

        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            byte strNo = (byte)(i + 1);
            if (occupiedStrings != null && occupiedStrings.Contains(strNo)) continue;

            if ((pitches[i] % 12) == targetPc)
            {
                int dist = Math.Abs(pitches[i] - noteNumber);
                if (dist < minDistance)
                {
                    minDistance = dist;
                    bestString = strNo;
                }
            }
        }

        if (bestString != 0) return bestString;

        // 3. 兜底: 在未占用琴弦中选择绝对音高最接近的
        bestString = 0;
        minDistance = int.MaxValue;
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            byte strNo = (byte)(i + 1);
            if (occupiedStrings != null && occupiedStrings.Contains(strNo)) continue;

            int dist = Math.Abs(pitches[i] - noteNumber);
            if (dist < minDistance)
            {
                minDistance = dist;
                bestString = strNo;
            }
        }

        if (bestString != 0) return bestString;

        // 若 6 弦全被占用，返回绝对音高最近的琴弦
        int absMinDist = int.MaxValue;
        byte fallbackStr = 1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
        {
            int dist = Math.Abs(pitches[i] - noteNumber);
            if (dist < absMinDist)
            {
                absMinDist = dist;
                fallbackStr = (byte)(i + 1);
            }
        }
        return fallbackStr;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 从原始 MIDI 音符与和弦切换流中提取按键拨弦事件 (支持琴弦同按、长按延音与 VoicingProfile)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<ChordPluckEvent> ExtractPluckEvents(
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawNotes,
        IReadOnlyList<ChordChangeEvent> chordEvents,
        ChordVoicingProfile? voicingProfile = null)
    {
        if (rawNotes == null || rawNotes.Count == 0)
            return new List<ChordPluckEvent>();

        voicingProfile ??= new ChordVoicingProfile();
        var notes = rawNotes.Where(n => n.Channel != 9).OrderBy(n => n.StartUs).ToList();
        if (notes.Count == 0) return new List<ChordPluckEvent>();

        var sortedChords = (chordEvents != null)
            ? chordEvents.OrderBy(c => c.TimeUs).ToList()
            : new List<ChordChangeEvent>();

        var plucks = new List<ChordPluckEvent>(notes.Count);

        // 同一时刻 (<= 15ms) 聚类，避免同时发声的音符抢占同一根弦 (琴弦同按支持)
        int i = 0;
        while (i < notes.Count)
        {
            int j = i;
            long clusterTime = notes[i].StartUs;
            var clusterNotes = new List<RawMidiNote>();

            while (j < notes.Count && (notes[j].StartUs - clusterTime) <= 15_000)
            {
                clusterNotes.Add(notes[j]);
                j++;
            }

            // 查找该时刻的生效和弦
            ChordId activeChord = ChordId.G;
            for (int k = sortedChords.Count - 1; k >= 0; k--)
            {
                if (sortedChords[k].TimeUs <= clusterTime)
                {
                    activeChord = sortedChords[k].Chord;
                    break;
                }
            }

            // 对簇内音符按音高从高到低分配琴弦 (高音对应细弦1，低音对应粗弦6)
            clusterNotes.Sort((a, b) => b.NoteNumber.CompareTo(a.NoteNumber));
            var occupied = new HashSet<byte>();

            foreach (var n in clusterNotes)
            {
                byte strNo = voicingProfile.MatchNoteToString(n.NoteNumber, activeChord, occupied, out _);
                occupied.Add(strNo);

                plucks.Add(new ChordPluckEvent(
                    timeUs: n.StartUs,
                    stringNo: strNo,
                    noteNumber: n.NoteNumber,
                    velocity: n.Velocity,
                    durationUs: n.DurationUs > 0 ? n.DurationUs : 300_000
                ));
            }

            i = j;
        }

        return plucks;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 攻击形态判定算法 (方案 6.3 & 6.4: Block / Strum / Arpeggio / Auto)
    /// </summary>
    public static (ChordArticulation Articulation, StrumDirectionHint Direction) DetectAttackArticulation(
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> cluster)
    {
        if (cluster == null || cluster.Count == 0)
            return (ChordArticulation.Auto, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);

        if (cluster.Count == 1)
        {
            return (ChordArticulation.Arpeggio, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
        }

        long minStart = cluster.Min(n => n.StartUs);
        long maxStart = cluster.Max(n => n.StartUs);
        long attackSpanUs = maxStart - minStart;

        // 若 >= 3 个音，AttackSpan <= 20ms: Block (齐奏块和弦)
        if (cluster.Count >= 3 && attackSpanUs <= 20_000)
        {
            return (ChordArticulation.Block, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
        }

        // 若 >= 3 个音，20ms < AttackSpan <= 100ms 且音高具有单调趋势: Strum
        if (cluster.Count >= 3 && attackSpanUs > 20_000 && attackSpanUs <= 100_000)
        {
            var ordered = cluster.OrderBy(n => n.StartUs).ToList();
            int asc = 0, desc = 0;
            for (int k = 0; k < ordered.Count - 1; k++)
            {
                int diff = ordered[k + 1].NoteNumber - ordered[k].NoteNumber;
                if (diff > 0) asc++;
                else if (diff < 0) desc++;
            }
            if (asc > desc) return (ChordArticulation.Strum, StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh);
            if (desc > asc) return (ChordArticulation.Strum, StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow);
            return (ChordArticulation.Strum, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
        }

        // 若为 2 个音且起音时间相差极小 (<= 20ms)，作为双音齐奏 Block 处理
        if (cluster.Count == 2 && attackSpanUs <= 20_000)
        {
            return (ChordArticulation.Block, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
        }

        // 若音符起音分散 (例如间距 > 40ms)，判定为分解和弦 / 琶音
        if (attackSpanUs > 40_000)
        {
            return (ChordArticulation.Arpeggio, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
        }

        return (ChordArticulation.Auto, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 全曲尝试 -11 ~ +11 半音统一转调，计算在游戏 24 和弦集合中的覆盖率 (方案 6.6)
    /// </summary>
    public static (int BestTranspose, double Coverage, Dictionary<int, double> AllTransposes) FindBestKeyTransposeFor24Chords(
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawNotes)
    {
        var result = new Dictionary<int, double>();
        if (rawNotes == null || rawNotes.Count == 0) return (0, 1.0, result);

        var notes = rawNotes.Where(n => n.Channel != 9).ToList();
        if (notes.Count == 0) return (0, 1.0, result);

        int bestTranspose = 0;
        double maxCoverage = -1.0;

        for (int t = -11; t <= 11; t++)
        {
            var shiftedNotes = notes.Select(n => new RawMidiNote
            {
                NoteNumber = (byte)Math.Clamp(n.NoteNumber + t, 0, 127),
                StartUs = n.StartUs,
                DurationUs = n.DurationUs,
                Velocity = n.Velocity,
                Channel = n.Channel
            }).ToList();

            var events = Analyze(shiftedNotes);
            if (events.Count == 0)
            {
                result[t] = 0.0;
                continue;
            }

            double avgConfidence = events.Average(e => e.Confidence);
            result[t] = avgConfidence;

            if (avgConfidence > maxCoverage)
            {
                maxCoverage = avgConfidence;
                bestTranspose = t;
            }
        }

        return (bestTranspose, maxCoverage, result);
    }
}

```

## `Guitar/ChordAnalyzerV2.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;
using MidiAutoPlayer.Midi;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar
{
    public sealed class ChordAnalyzerV2Options
    {
        public ChordRecognitionMode Mode { get; set; } = ChordRecognitionMode.Loose;
        /// <summary>是否启用自动半音移调（强烈建议开启，将任意调号对齐到游戏 C/G 核心和弦池）</summary>
        public bool EnableAutoTranspose { get; set; } = true;

        /// <summary>Attack grouping window; staggered same-track strings may join within 25ms.</summary>
        public long AttackClusterUs { get; set; } = 0;

        /// <summary>和声观察窗口：320ms（覆盖约半拍到一拍的和声延续）</summary>
        public long HarmonicWindowUs { get; set; } = 320_000;

        /// <summary>和弦最小稳定保持时间：240ms（防止短促装饰音引发频繁切槽）</summary>
        public long MinChordStabilityUs { get; set; } = 240_000;

        /// <summary>切换迟滞阈值：新和弦得分需超过旧和弦至少 0.14 才切换</summary>
        public double HysteresisMargin { get; set; } = 0.14;
    }

    public static class ChordAnalyzerV2
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// 游戏内置 24 个和弦在 6~1 弦（低音 6 弦 -> 高音 1 弦）的标准发声 MIDI 音高表
        /// 用于和弦模式下精准分配拨弦（1~6 键）及判断单音是否可直接借用当前和弦弹出
        /// </summary>
        public static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<ChordId, byte[]> ChordStringPitches =
            new Dictionary<ChordId, byte[]>
            {
                // P1: G, Am, Bm, C, D, Em
                [ChordId.G]      = new byte[] { 43, 47, 50, 55, 59, 67 }, // 6弦->1弦: G2,B2,D3,G3,B3,G4
                [ChordId.Am]     = new byte[] { 45, 45, 52, 57, 60, 64 }, // A2,E3,A3,C4,E4
                [ChordId.Bm]     = new byte[] { 47, 47, 54, 59, 62, 66 }, // B2,F#3,B3,D4,F#4
                [ChordId.C]      = new byte[] { 48, 48, 52, 55, 60, 64 }, // C3,E3,G3,C4,E4
                [ChordId.D]      = new byte[] { 50, 45, 50, 57, 62, 66 }, // D3,A3,D4,F#4
                [ChordId.Em]     = new byte[] { 40, 47, 52, 55, 59, 64 }, // E2,B2,E3,G3,B3,E4

                // P2: Cmaj7, C7, Cm7, G7, C9, Cm9
                [ChordId.Cmaj7]  = new byte[] { 48, 48, 52, 55, 59, 64 },
                [ChordId.C7]     = new byte[] { 48, 48, 52, 58, 60, 64 },
                [ChordId.Cm7]    = new byte[] { 48, 48, 55, 58, 63, 67 },
                [ChordId.G7]     = new byte[] { 43, 47, 50, 55, 59, 65 },
                [ChordId.C9]     = new byte[] { 48, 48, 52, 58, 62, 67 },
                [ChordId.Cm9]    = new byte[] { 48, 48, 51, 58, 62, 67 },

                // P3: C5, D5, G5, A5, Cmaj9, Cadd9
                [ChordId.C5]     = new byte[] { 48, 48, 55, 60, 60, 67 },
                [ChordId.D5]     = new byte[] { 50, 50, 57, 62, 62, 69 },
                [ChordId.G5]     = new byte[] { 43, 50, 55, 55, 62, 67 },
                [ChordId.A5]     = new byte[] { 45, 45, 52, 57, 57, 64 },
                [ChordId.Cmaj9]  = new byte[] { 48, 48, 52, 59, 62, 67 },
                [ChordId.Cadd9]  = new byte[] { 48, 48, 52, 55, 62, 64 },

                // P4: G/B, Am7/G, D/F#, F/A, A, Dm
                [ChordId.G_B]    = new byte[] { 47, 47, 50, 55, 62, 67 },
                [ChordId.Am7_G]  = new byte[] { 43, 45, 52, 55, 60, 64 },
                [ChordId.D_Fsharp] = new byte[] { 42, 45, 50, 57, 62, 66 },
                [ChordId.F_A]    = new byte[] { 45, 45, 53, 57, 60, 65 },
                [ChordId.A]      = new byte[] { 45, 45, 52, 57, 61, 64 },
                [ChordId.Dm]     = new byte[] { 50, 45, 50, 57, 62, 65 }
            };

        /// <summary>
        /// 自动寻找最佳半音移调量 (-6 ~ +5)，使歌曲音高完美落入游戏内置的 C/G 调和弦体系
        /// </summary>
        public static int FindBestSemitoneTranspose(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes)
        {
            if (notes == null || notes.Count == 0) return 0;

            // C 大调与 G 大调的核心自然音级集合: C(0), D(2), E(4), F(5), F#(6), G(7), A(9), B(11)
            // 其中 C(0), D(2), E(4), G(7), A(9), B(11) 权重最高
            double[] targetScaleWeights = new double[12]
            {
                1.0,  // 0: C
                -0.8, // 1: C# (仅 A 大调三音偶见)
                1.0,  // 2: D
                -0.6, // 3: Eb
                1.0,  // 4: E
                0.65, // 5: F (Dm, F/A, G7)
                0.65, // 6: F# (G, D, Bm)
                1.0,  // 7: G
                -1.0, // 8: Ab (24和弦完全无 Ab)
                1.0,  // 9: A
                -0.5, // 10: Bb
                0.95  // 11: B
            };

            double[] pcHistogram = new double[12];
            foreach (var n in notes)
            {
                if (n.Channel == 9) continue;
                double durWeight = Math.Clamp((n.DurationUs > 0 ? n.DurationUs : 250_000) / 100_000.0, 0.3, 8.0);
                double bassBoost = n.NoteNumber < 60 ? 1.8 : 1.0;
                pcHistogram[n.NoteNumber % 12] += durWeight * (n.Velocity / 127.0) * bassBoost;
            }

            int bestShift = 0;
            double bestScore = double.MinValue;

            for (int shift = -6; shift <= 5; shift++)
            {
                double score = 0;
                for (int pc = 0; pc < 12; pc++)
                {
                    int shiftedPc = ((pc + shift) % 12 + 12) % 12;
                    score += pcHistogram[pc] * targetScaleWeights[shiftedPc];
                }

                // 轻微偏好不移调（若原曲本就是 C/G 调，shift=0 直接胜出）
                score -= Math.Abs(shift) * 0.02 * pcHistogram.Sum();

                if (score > bestScore)
                {
                    bestScore = score;
                    bestShift = shift;
                }
            }

            return bestShift;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 重写后的高精度 MIDI 和弦识别主入口
        /// </summary>
        public static List<ChordEvent> AnalyzeDetailed(
            IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawNotes,
            out int appliedSemitoneShift,
            ChordAnalyzerV2Options? options = null)
        {
            appliedSemitoneShift = 0;
            if (rawNotes == null || rawNotes.Count == 0)
                return new List<ChordEvent>();

            options ??= new ChordAnalyzerV2Options();
            var filtered = rawNotes.Where(n => n.Channel != 9).OrderBy(n => n.StartUs).ToList();
            if (filtered.Count == 0)
                return new List<ChordEvent>();

            // 1. 自动半音移调对齐到 C/G 核心和弦池
            if (options.EnableAutoTranspose)
            {
                appliedSemitoneShift = FindBestSemitoneTranspose(filtered);
            }

            int shift = appliedSemitoneShift;
            var notes = (shift == 0)
                ? filtered
                : filtered.Select(n => PhraseOctaveFolder.CloneWithPitch(n,
                    Math.Clamp(n.NoteNumber + shift, 0, 127))).ToList();

            // 2. 起音聚类
            var clusters = ClusterAttacks(notes, options.AttackClusterUs);
            var results = new List<ChordEvent>(clusters.Count);

            ChordId currentChord = ChordId.Unknown;
            long lastSwitchTimeUs = -options.MinChordStabilityUs;
            int windowEndIndex = 0;
            var activeWindow = new LinkedList<RawMidiNote>();
            var expirations = new PriorityQueue<LinkedListNode<RawMidiNote>, long>();

            foreach (var cluster in clusters)
            {
                long attackTime = cluster[0].StartUs;
                long winEnd = attackTime + options.HarmonicWindowUs;

                double[] evidence = new double[12];
                double totalEvidence = 0;
                int lowestMidi = int.MaxValue;
                byte lowestBassPc = 255;
                var activeNotes = new List<RawMidiNote>();

                while (windowEndIndex < notes.Count && notes[windowEndIndex].StartUs < winEnd)
                {
                    var note = notes[windowEndIndex++];
                    var node = activeWindow.AddLast(note);
                    long noteEnd = note.StartUs + (note.DurationUs > 0 ? note.DurationUs : 250_000);
                    expirations.Enqueue(node, noteEnd);
                }
                while (expirations.TryPeek(out _, out long endUs) && endUs <= attackTime)
                    activeWindow.Remove(expirations.Dequeue());

                // 3. 声部加权证据收集（强化低音根音与长音，抑制高音短促旋律经过音）
                foreach (var n in activeWindow)
                {
                    long noteEnd = n.StartUs + (n.DurationUs > 0 ? n.DurationUs : 250_000);
                    if (n.StartUs >= winEnd || noteEnd <= attackTime) continue;

                    long overlap = Math.Min(noteEnd, winEnd) - Math.Max(n.StartUs, attackTime);
                    if (overlap <= 0) continue;

                    activeNotes.Add(n);

                    double durationRatio = overlap / (double)options.HarmonicWindowUs;
                    double velFactor = Math.Clamp(n.Velocity / 127.0, 0.35, 1.0);

                    // 关键改进：低音(<60)赋予 2.4 倍和声权重；高音短音(<120ms)视为旋律经过音，权重降至 0.35
                    double roleWeight = 1.0;
                    if (n.NoteNumber < 55) roleWeight = 2.4;
                    else if (n.NoteNumber < 64) roleWeight = 1.6;
                    else if (n.DurationUs < 120_000) roleWeight = 0.35;

                    double weight = durationRatio * velFactor * roleWeight;
                    byte pc = (byte)(n.NoteNumber % 12);
                    evidence[pc] += weight;
                    totalEvidence += weight;

                    // 仅用中低音区或足够长的音作为有效低音判据
                    if (n.NoteNumber < lowestMidi && (n.NoteNumber <= 64 || cluster.Count == 1))
                    {
                        lowestMidi = n.NoteNumber;
                        lowestBassPc = pc;
                    }
                }

                if (totalEvidence < 0.04) continue;

                // 4. 对 24 个内置和弦进行分层打分
                var (bestChord, bestScore, currentScore) = Score24ChordsEnhanced(
                    evidence, totalEvidence, lowestBassPc, currentChord, options.Mode);

                // 5. 防抖稳定区判断
                bool canSwitch = (currentChord == ChordId.Unknown)
                    || (bestChord != currentChord
                        && (attackTime - lastSwitchTimeUs >= options.MinChordStabilityUs || bestScore >= currentScore + 0.32)
                        && bestScore >= currentScore + options.HysteresisMargin);

                if (canSwitch && bestChord != ChordId.Unknown)
                {
                    currentChord = bestChord;
                    lastSwitchTimeUs = attackTime;
                }

                ChordId finalChord = (currentChord != ChordId.Unknown) ? currentChord : bestChord;
                var def = ChordAnalyzer.GetDefinition(finalChord);

                // 6. 识别演奏形态与上下扫方向
                var (articulation, direction) = ClassifyArticulationAndDirection(cluster, activeNotes);

                results.Add(new ChordEvent
                {
                    StartUs = attackTime,
                    EndUs = winEnd,
                    Chord = finalChord,
                    Page = def?.Page ?? 1,
                    Slot = def?.Slot ?? 1,
                    Confidence = Math.Clamp(bestScore, 0.0, 1.0),
                    SourceNotes = activeNotes,
                    AttackNotes = cluster,
                    Articulation = articulation,
                    Direction = direction
                });
            }

            return results;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 改进版 24 和弦评分器：修复五度和弦分母漏洞与九和弦误判
        /// </summary>
        public static (ChordId BestChord, double BestScore, double CurrentScore) Score24ChordsEnhanced(
            double[] evidence,
            double totalEvidence,
            byte lowestBassPc,
            ChordId currentChord,
            ChordRecognitionMode mode = ChordRecognitionMode.Loose)
        {
            ChordId bestChord = ChordId.C;
            double bestScore = double.MinValue;
            double currentScore = 0;

            foreach (var def in ChordAnalyzer.SupportedChords)
            {
                double inChordEvidence = 0;
                double outChordEvidence = 0;
                int presentCount = 0;

                for (byte pc = 0; pc < 12; pc++)
                {
                    if (def.PitchClasses.Contains(pc))
                    {
                        inChordEvidence += evidence[pc];
                        if (evidence[pc] > 0.04 * totalEvidence) presentCount++;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        outChordEvidence += evidence[pc];
                    }
                }

                double coverage = inChordEvidence / Math.Max(0.001, totalEvidence);
                // 修复五度和弦漏洞：分母至少按 3 音三和弦归一化，避免 2 音五度和弦轻易拿满分
                double completeness = presentCount / Math.Max(3.0, def.PitchClasses.Count);

                // 根音与低音匹配奖励
                double rootBonus = (evidence[def.RootPitchClass] > 0.08 * totalEvidence) ? 0.16 : -0.10;
                double bassBonus = 0;
                if (def.SpecialBassPitchClass.HasValue)
                {
                    bassBonus = (lowestBassPc == def.SpecialBassPitchClass.Value) ? 0.32 : -0.50;
                }
                else if (lowestBassPc == def.RootPitchClass)
                {
                    bassBonus = 0.22;
                }

                // 和弦层级先验（优先选用 P1 基础三和弦与 P4 常用和弦，抑制无故跳 P2/P3 复杂和弦）
                double tierPrior = def.Page switch
                {
                    1 => 0.12, // P1 (G, Am, Bm, C, D, Em): 最高优先级，0翻页成本
                    4 => 0.06, // P4 (Dm, A, F/A 等): 常用自然和弦
                    2 => -0.06, // P2 (七/九和弦): 仅在七音/九音证据充足时胜出
                    3 => def.PitchClasses.Count == 2 ? -0.14 : -0.05, // P3 五度和弦施加强抑制，防空洞感
                    _ => 0.0
                };

                double score = 0.62 * coverage
                             + 0.26 * completeness
                             + rootBonus
                             + bassBonus
                             + tierPrior
                             - 0.35 * (outChordEvidence / Math.Max(0.001, totalEvidence));
                if (mode == ChordRecognitionMode.Strict)
                    score -= 0.30 * (outChordEvidence / Math.Max(0.001, totalEvidence));
                else if (mode == ChordRecognitionMode.BassRoot)
                    score += bassBonus * 0.75;

                if (def.PitchClasses.Count == 2)
                {
                    int minorThird = (def.RootPitchClass + 3) % 12;
                    int majorThird = (def.RootPitchClass + 4) % 12;
                    if (evidence[minorThird] + evidence[majorThird] > 0.08 * totalEvidence)
                        score -= 0.35;
                }

                int extensionPc = def.Id switch
                {
                    ChordId.Cmaj7 => 11,
                    ChordId.C7 or ChordId.Cm7 => 10,
                    ChordId.G7 => 5,
                    ChordId.C9 or ChordId.Cm9 or ChordId.Cmaj9 or ChordId.Cadd9 => 2,
                    _ => -1
                };
                if (extensionPc >= 0 && evidence[extensionPc] < 0.08 * totalEvidence)
                    score -= 0.28;

                if (def.Id == currentChord)
                {
                    score += 0.12;
                    currentScore = score;
                }

                if (score > bestScore)
                {
                    bestScore = score;
                    bestChord = def.Id;
                }
            }

            return (bestChord, bestScore, currentScore);
        }

        internal static (ChordArticulation Articulation, StrumDirectionHint Direction) ClassifyArticulationAndDirection(
            IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> cluster,
            IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> activeNotes)
        {
            if (cluster.Count == 1)
            {
                return (ChordArticulation.Arpeggio, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
            }

            long spanUs = cluster.Max(n => n.StartUs) - cluster.Min(n => n.StartUs);

            // >= 3 个音同时或微时差进入：优先使用扫弦 (Strum)，比死板的数字键齐按 (Block) 更有吉他真实感
            if (cluster.Count >= 3)
            {
                var ordered = cluster.OrderBy(n => n.StartUs).ThenBy(n => n.NoteNumber).ToList();
                int asc = 0, desc = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < ordered.Count - 1; i++)
                {
                    int diff = ordered[i + 1].NoteNumber - ordered[i].NoteNumber;
                    if (diff > 0) asc++;
                    else if (diff < 0) desc++;
                }

                if (spanUs >= 12_000)
                {
                    if (asc > desc) return (ChordArticulation.Strum, StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh); // 低音到高音 -> 下扫(右键)
                    if (desc > asc) return (ChordArticulation.Strum, StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow); // 高音到低音 -> 上扫(左键)
                }

                return (ChordArticulation.Strum, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
            }

            // 双音齐奏 (如根音+高音旋律)：采用 Block 双弦同时点按
            if (cluster.Count == 2 && spanUs <= 22_000)
            {
                return (ChordArticulation.Block, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
            }

            return (ChordArticulation.Arpeggio, StrumDirectionHint.Unknown);
        }

        private static List<List<RawMidiNote>> ClusterAttacks(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> sortedNotes, long windowUs)
        {
            var clusters = new List<List<RawMidiNote>>();
            int i = 0;
            while (i < sortedNotes.Count)
            {
                long start = sortedNotes[i].StartUs;
                var batch = new List<RawMidiNote> { sortedNotes[i] };
                int j = i + 1;
                while (j < sortedNotes.Count && (sortedNotes[j].StartUs - start) <= windowUs)
                {
                    if (!AttackClusterPolicy.CanJoin(batch, sortedNotes[j], start,
                            windowUs * 25 / 30, GuitarTuning.Standard))
                        break;

                    batch.Add(sortedNotes[j]);
                    j++;
                }
                clusters.Add(batch);
                i = j;
            }
            return clusters;
        }
    }
}


```

## `Guitar/AttackClusterPolicy.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

internal static class AttackClusterPolicy
{
    public static bool CanJoin(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> frame, RawMidiNote note, long frameStartUs,
        long sameTrackRollWindowUs, GuitarTuning tuning)
    {
        if (note.StartUs <= frameStartUs || frame.All(existing => existing.TrackId != note.TrackId))
            return true;

        if (note.StartUs - frameStartUs > sameTrackRollWindowUs)
            return false;

        var sameTrack = frame.Where(existing => existing.TrackId == note.TrackId).ToList();
        bool markedBrush = note.IsBrushChord && sameTrack.All(existing => existing.IsBrushChord);
        if (!markedBrush && sameTrack.Any(existing => existing.NoteNumber == note.NoteNumber))
            return false;

        if (note.OrigString is int sourceString &&
            sameTrack.Any(existing => existing.OrigString == sourceString))
            return false;

        // Source strings distinguish GTP retriggers, but transposition may move a
        // pitch to another playable string. Assign target strings from final pitches.
        sameTrack.Add(note);
        if (sameTrack.Count > 6) return false;
        var used = new bool[7];
        return CanAssign(sameTrack, 0, used, tuning);
    }

    private static bool CanAssign(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes, int index, bool[] used, GuitarTuning tuning)
    {
        if (index == notes.Count) return true;
        var note = notes[index];
        for (int stringNo = 1; stringNo <= 6; stringNo++)
        {
            if (used[stringNo])
                continue;
            int fret = note.NoteNumber - tuning.GetOpenMidiNote((byte)stringNo);
            if (fret is < 0 or > 24) continue;
            used[stringNo] = true;
            if (CanAssign(notes, index + 1, used, tuning)) return true;
            used[stringNo] = false;
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

## `Guitar/AccompanimentEventFilter.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

/// <summary>
/// Rebuilds chord accompaniment as a beat-driven guitar part. Harmony is held
/// for a bar half, while the right hand follows a small set of rhythmic grooves.
/// </summary>
internal static class AccompanimentEventFilter
{
    private static readonly byte[] ArpeggioPattern = [6, 3, 2, 3, 1, 3, 2, 3];

    public static List<ChordEvent> Prepare(
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> sourceNotes,
        IReadOnlyList<ChordEvent> rawChordEvents,
        int semitoneShift,
        double speedMultiplier = 1.0)
    {
        if (sourceNotes.Count == 0 || rawChordEvents.Count == 0)
            return rawChordEvents.ToList();

        double speed = Math.Max(0.1, speedMultiplier);
        var scaledNotes = sourceNotes.Select(note => new RawMidiNote
        {
            StartUs = (long)Math.Round(note.StartUs / speed),
            DurationUs = Math.Max(20_000, (long)Math.Round(note.DurationUs / speed)),
            NoteNumber = note.Channel == 9
                ? note.NoteNumber
                : (byte)Math.Clamp(note.NoteNumber + semitoneShift, 0, 127),
            Velocity = note.Velocity,
            Channel = note.Channel,
            TrackId = note.TrackId,
            Program = note.Program,
            BarIndex = note.BarIndex,
            BeatInBar = note.BeatInBar,
            BeatsPerBar = note.BeatsPerBar,
            BeatUnitDenominator = note.BeatUnitDenominator,
            HasMeterPosition = note.HasMeterPosition,
            IsBrushChord = note.IsBrushChord,
            IsBrushUp = note.IsBrushUp,
            SourceFormat = note.SourceFormat
        }).OrderBy(note => note.StartUs).ToList();

        var bars = BuildBarGrid(scaledNotes);
        var chords = rawChordEvents.Where(ev => ev.Chord != ChordId.Unknown)
            .OrderBy(ev => ev.StartUs).ToList();
        if (bars.Count == 0 || chords.Count == 0) return rawChordEvents.ToList();

        var plans = BuildHarmonicPlans(bars, chords);
        var result = new List<ChordEvent>(bars.Count * 6);
        for (int barIndex = 0; barIndex < plans.Count; barIndex++)
        {
            var plan = plans[barIndex];
            var bar = plan.Bar;
            var activeNotes = scaledNotes.Where(note =>
                note.StartUs < bar.EndUs && note.StartUs + note.DurationUs > bar.StartUs).ToList();
            if (activeNotes.Count == 0) continue;

            var onsets = scaledNotes.Where(note =>
                note.StartUs >= bar.StartUs - 15_000 && note.StartUs < bar.EndUs).ToList();
            int beats = Math.Clamp((int)bar.BeatsPerBar, 2, 6);
            int slotCount = beats * 2;
            long slotDurationUs = Math.Max(80_000, (bar.EndUs - bar.StartUs) / slotCount);
            int energy = EvaluateBarEnergy(onsets, beats);
            ChordId nextBarChord = barIndex + 1 < plans.Count
                ? plans[barIndex + 1].FirstHalfChord
                : plan.SecondHalfChord;

            for (int slot = 0; slot < slotCount; slot++)
            {
                long slotStartUs = bar.StartUs + slot * slotDurationUs;
                long slotEndUs = Math.Min(bar.EndUs, slotStartUs + slotDurationUs);
                bool secondHalf = slot >= slotCount / 2;
                ChordId chord = secondHalf ? plan.SecondHalfChord : plan.FirstHalfChord;
                if (chord == ChordId.Unknown) continue;

                ChordId nextChord = slot == slotCount - 1
                    ? nextBarChord
                    : slot == slotCount / 2 - 1 ? plan.SecondHalfChord : chord;
                bool chordChangesNext = nextChord != chord;
                if (chordChangesNext && slot % 2 == 1 && slotDurationUs < 320_000)
                    continue;

                bool hasActiveNote = activeNotes.Any(note =>
                    note.StartUs < slotEndUs && note.StartUs + note.DurationUs > slotStartUs);
                bool hasAttack = onsets.Any(note =>
                    note.StartUs >= slotStartUs - 25_000 && note.StartUs < slotEndUs - 10_000);
                if (!hasActiveNote || (energy == 0 && !hasAttack)) continue;

                var definition = ChordAnalyzer.GetDefinition(chord);
                if (definition == null || !GameChordGroundTruth.Table.TryGetValue(chord, out var truth))
                    continue;

                ChordEvent? generated = BuildSlotEvent(
                    slot, slotCount, slotStartUs, slotEndUs, chord,
                    definition.Page, definition.Slot, definition.RootPitchClass,
                    truth.SafeCleanStrings, truth.ActualMidi1To6, energy, hasAttack);
                if (generated != null) result.Add(generated);
            }
        }

        return result.Count > 0 ? result : rawChordEvents.ToList();
    }

    private sealed record BarSlice(long Index, long StartUs, long EndUs, byte BeatsPerBar);
    private sealed record BarPlan(BarSlice Bar, ChordId FirstHalfChord, ChordId SecondHalfChord);

    private static List<BarPlan> BuildHarmonicPlans(IReadOnlyList<BarSlice> bars, IReadOnlyList<ChordEvent> chords)
    {
        var plans = new List<BarPlan>(bars.Count);
        ChordId previousChord = chords[0].Chord;
        foreach (var bar in bars)
        {
            long midpoint = bar.StartUs + (bar.EndUs - bar.StartUs) / 2;
            ChordId first = ResolveDominantChord(chords, bar.StartUs, midpoint, previousChord);
            ChordId second = ResolveDominantChord(chords, midpoint, bar.EndUs, first);
            if (bar.EndUs - bar.StartUs < 1_000_000) second = first;
            plans.Add(new BarPlan(bar, first, second));
            previousChord = second;
        }
        return plans;
    }

    private static List<BarSlice> BuildBarGrid(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes)
    {
        if (notes.Count == 0) return [];
        var pitched = notes.Where(note => note.Channel != 9).ToList();
        if (pitched.Count == 0) pitched = notes.ToList();

        long firstUs = pitched.Min(note => note.StartUs);
        long lastUs = pitched.Max(note => note.StartUs + Math.Max(200_000, note.DurationUs));
        long estimatedBeatUs = EstimateBeatDurationUs(pitched);

        if (pitched.Any(note => note.HasMeterPosition))
        {
            var measured = pitched.Where(note => note.HasMeterPosition)
                .GroupBy(note => note.BarIndex).OrderBy(group => group.Key).ToList();
            if (measured.Count >= 2)
            {
                var result = new List<BarSlice>();
                long firstBar = measured[0].Key;
                long lastBar = measured[^1].Key;
                int measuredIndex = 0;
                byte beats = measured[0].First().BeatsPerBar is >= 2 and <= 6
                    ? measured[0].First().BeatsPerBar : (byte)4;
                long cursor = Math.Max(0, measured[0].Min(note =>
                    note.StartUs - (long)Math.Round(note.BeatInBar * estimatedBeatUs)));
                for (long index = firstBar; index <= lastBar && result.Count < 100_000; index++)
                {
                    if (measuredIndex < measured.Count && measured[measuredIndex].Key == index)
                    {
                        var group = measured[measuredIndex++];
                        var anchor = group.OrderBy(note => note.BeatInBar).ThenBy(note => note.StartUs).First();
                        beats = anchor.BeatsPerBar is >= 2 and <= 6 ? anchor.BeatsPerBar : beats;
                        long groupStart = Math.Max(0, anchor.StartUs - (long)Math.Round(anchor.BeatInBar * estimatedBeatUs));
                        if (groupStart > cursor) cursor = groupStart;
                    }

                    long end = cursor + beats * estimatedBeatUs;
                    result.Add(new BarSlice(index, cursor, end, beats));
                    cursor = end;
                }
                if (result.Count > 0) return result;
            }
        }

        long barDurationUs = estimatedBeatUs * 4;
        var fallback = new List<BarSlice>();
        long start = firstUs;
        long indexFallback = 0;
        while (start < lastUs && fallback.Count < 100_000)
        {
            fallback.Add(new BarSlice(indexFallback++, start, start + barDurationUs, 4));
            start += barDurationUs;
        }
        return fallback;
    }

    private static long EstimateBeatDurationUs(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes)
    {
        var onsets = notes.Select(note => note.StartUs).Distinct().Order().ToList();
        var intervals = new List<long>();
        for (int i = 1; i < onsets.Count; i++)
        {
            long interval = onsets[i] - onsets[i - 1];
            while (interval > 0 && interval < 340_000) interval *= 2;
            while (interval > 760_000) interval /= 2;
            if (interval is >= 340_000 and <= 760_000) intervals.Add(interval);
        }
        if (intervals.Count == 0) return 500_000;
        intervals.Sort();
        return intervals[intervals.Count / 2];
    }

    private static ChordId ResolveDominantChord(
        IReadOnlyList<ChordEvent> chords, long startUs, long endUs, ChordId fallback)
    {
        var weights = new Dictionary<ChordId, double>();
        for (int i = 0; i < chords.Count; i++)
        {
            var chord = chords[i];
            long chordEnd = i + 1 < chords.Count ? chords[i + 1].StartUs : chord.EndUs;
            long overlap = Math.Min(chordEnd, endUs) - Math.Max(chord.StartUs, startUs);
            if (overlap <= 0) continue;
            double weight = overlap * Math.Max(0.3, chord.Confidence);
            if (Math.Abs(chord.StartUs - startUs) <= 45_000) weight *= 1.6;
            weights[chord.Chord] = weights.GetValueOrDefault(chord.Chord) + weight;
        }
        if (weights.Count > 0) return weights.OrderByDescending(pair => pair.Value).First().Key;
        return chords.LastOrDefault(chord => chord.StartUs <= startUs)?.Chord ?? fallback;
    }

    private static int EvaluateBarEnergy(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> onsets, int beats)
    {
        var pitched = onsets.Where(note => note.Channel != 9).OrderBy(note => note.StartUs).ToList();
        int onsetGroups = CountOnsetGroups(pitched, 35_000);
        int drumHits = onsets.Count(note => note.Channel == 9);
        if (onsetGroups <= 1) return drumHits >= beats ? 3 : 0;

        int multiNoteGroups = CountMultiNoteGroups(pitched, 35_000);
        double averageVelocity = onsets.Count == 0 ? 70 : onsets.Average(note => (double)note.Velocity);
        if (drumHits >= beats || (multiNoteGroups >= 2 && onsetGroups >= beats + 1) ||
            (onsetGroups >= beats * 2 && averageVelocity >= 88))
            return 3;
        if (onsetGroups >= beats && (multiNoteGroups > 0 || pitched.Any(note => note.NoteNumber < 55)))
            return 2;
        return 1;
    }

    private static int CountOnsetGroups(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes, long toleranceUs)
    {
        int count = 0;
        long last = long.MinValue / 2;
        foreach (var note in notes)
        {
            if (note.StartUs - last <= toleranceUs) continue;
            count++;
            last = note.StartUs;
        }
        return count;
    }

    private static int CountMultiNoteGroups(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes, long toleranceUs)
    {
        int count = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < notes.Count;)
        {
            int end = i + 1;
            while (end < notes.Count && notes[end].StartUs - notes[i].StartUs <= toleranceUs) end++;
            if (end - i >= 3 || notes.Skip(i).Take(end - i).Any(note => note.IsBrushChord)) count++;
            i = end;
        }
        return count;
    }

    private static ChordEvent? BuildSlotEvent(
        int slot, int totalSlots, long startUs, long endUs, ChordId chord,
        byte page, byte chordSlot, byte rootPitchClass, IReadOnlyList<byte> safeStrings,
        IReadOnlyList<byte> pitches1To6, int energy, bool hasMidiAttack)
    {
        if (safeStrings.Count == 0) return null;
        if (energy == 0)
        {
            if (slot == 0 || (slot == totalSlots / 2 && hasMidiAttack))
                return CreateStrumEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot,
                    StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh, safeStrings, pitches1To6);
            return null;
        }

        if (energy == 1)
        {
            byte stringNo = SelectArpeggioString(ArpeggioPattern[slot % ArpeggioPattern.Length], safeStrings, pitches1To6, rootPitchClass);
            return CreatePluckEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot, stringNo, pitches1To6[stringNo - 1],
                (byte)(slot % 4 == 0 ? 96 : 78));
        }

        if (energy == 2)
        {
            if (slot == 0 || slot == totalSlots / 2)
            {
                byte bass = SelectBassString(safeStrings, pitches1To6, rootPitchClass);
                return CreatePluckEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot, bass,
                    pitches1To6[bass - 1], 100);
            }
            if (slot is 2 or 6)
                return CreateStrumEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot,
                    StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh, safeStrings, pitches1To6);
            if (slot is 3 or 7 && hasMidiAttack)
                return CreateStrumEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot,
                    StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow, safeStrings, pitches1To6);
            return null;
        }

        int patternSlot = slot % 8;
        if (patternSlot is 0 or 2 or 6)
            return CreateStrumEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot,
                StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh, safeStrings, pitches1To6);
        if (patternSlot is 3 or 5 or 7)
            return CreateStrumEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot,
                StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow, safeStrings, pitches1To6);
        if (patternSlot == 4 && hasMidiAttack)
            return CreateStrumEvent(startUs, endUs, chord, page, chordSlot,
                StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh, safeStrings, pitches1To6);
        return null;
    }

    private static byte SelectArpeggioString(
        byte desired, IReadOnlyList<byte> safeStrings, IReadOnlyList<byte> pitches, byte rootPitchClass)
    {
        if (safeStrings.Contains(desired)) return desired;
        return safeStrings.OrderBy(stringNo => Math.Abs(stringNo - desired))
            .ThenByDescending(stringNo => (byte)(pitches[stringNo - 1] % 12) == rootPitchClass)
            .First();
    }

    private static byte SelectBassString(
        IReadOnlyList<byte> safeStrings, IReadOnlyList<byte> pitches, byte rootPitchClass)
    {
        return safeStrings.Where(stringNo => pitches[stringNo - 1] % 12 == rootPitchClass)
            .DefaultIfEmpty(safeStrings.Max())
            .Max();
    }

    private static ChordEvent CreatePluckEvent(
        long startUs, long endUs, ChordId chord, byte page, byte chordSlot,
        byte stringNo, byte pitch, byte velocity)
    {
        var note = new RawMidiNote
        {
            StartUs = startUs,
            DurationUs = Math.Max(40_000, endUs - startUs - 10_000),
            NoteNumber = pitch,
            Velocity = velocity,
            OrigString = stringNo
        };
        return new ChordEvent
        {
            StartUs = startUs,
            EndUs = endUs,
            Chord = chord,
            Page = page,
            Slot = chordSlot,
            Confidence = 0.95,
            SourceNotes = [note],
            AttackNotes = [note],
            Articulation = ChordArticulation.Arpeggio
        };
    }

    private static ChordEvent CreateStrumEvent(
        long startUs, long endUs, ChordId chord, byte page, byte chordSlot,
        StrumDirectionHint direction, IReadOnlyList<byte> safeStrings, IReadOnlyList<byte> pitches1To6)
    {
        IEnumerable<byte> ordered = direction == StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow
            ? safeStrings.Order()
            : safeStrings.OrderDescending();
        var strings = ordered.Take(4).ToList();
        var notes = strings.Select((stringNo, index) => new RawMidiNote
        {
            StartUs = startUs + index * 8_000,
            DurationUs = Math.Max(60_000, endUs - startUs - 10_000),
            NoteNumber = pitches1To6[stringNo - 1],
            Velocity = (byte)(direction == StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh ? 95 : 82),
            OrigString = stringNo
        }).ToList();
        if (notes.Count == 0) return null!;
        return new ChordEvent
        {
            StartUs = startUs,
            EndUs = endUs,
            Chord = chord,
            Page = page,
            Slot = chordSlot,
            Confidence = 0.95,
            SourceNotes = notes,
            AttackNotes = notes,
            Articulation = ChordArticulation.Strum,
            Direction = direction
        };
    }
}
```

## `Guitar/GameChordGroundTruth.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

/// <summary>
/// Game chord-wheel pitches and safe-string masks recovered from chord.json.
/// String arrays are ordered by physical string number: 1 (highest) through 6 (lowest).
/// This table is consumed by hybrid mode only; chord accompaniment keeps its own compiler.
/// </summary>
public static class GameChordGroundTruth
{
    public static readonly byte[] OpenStringMidi1To6 = [64, 59, 55, 50, 45, 40];

    public sealed record ChordGroundTruthInfo(
        ChordId Id,
        string Name,
        byte Page,
        byte Slot,
        sbyte[] Offsets1To6,
        byte[] ActualMidi1To6,
        byte[] SafeCleanStrings,
        bool CanUseFullMouseStrum);

    public static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<ChordId, ChordGroundTruthInfo> Table;

    static GameChordGroundTruth()
    {
        var definitions = new (ChordId Id, string Name, byte Page, byte Slot, sbyte[] Offsets, byte[] SafeStrings, bool FullStrum)[]
        {
            (ChordId.G, "G", 1, 1, [3, 0, 0, 0, 2, 3], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], true),
            (ChordId.Am, "Am", 1, 2, [0, 0, 2, 2, 1, 0], [1, 2, 3, 4, 6], false),
            (ChordId.Bm, "Bm", 1, 3, [2, 2, 4, 4, 3, 2], [1, 2, 3, 4, 6], false),
            (ChordId.C, "C", 1, 4, [0, 1, 0, 2, 8, 0], [1, 2, 3, 4], false),
            (ChordId.D, "D", 1, 5, [2, 4, 2, 0, 0, 0], [1, 3, 4, 5], false),
            (ChordId.Em, "Em", 1, 6, [0, 2, 2, 0, 0, 0], [1, 4, 6], false),

            (ChordId.Cmaj7, "Cmaj7", 2, 1, [0, 0, 0, 2, 8, 0], [1, 2, 3, 4], false),
            (ChordId.C7, "C7", 2, 2, [0, 1, 3, 2, 8, 0], [1, 2, 3, 4], false),
            (ChordId.Cm7, "Cm7", 2, 3, [4, 4, 3, 10, 8, 4], [2, 3, 4], false),
            (ChordId.G7, "G7", 2, 4, [1, 0, 0, 0, 2, 3], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], true),
            (ChordId.C9, "C9", 2, 5, [4, 4, 3, 2, 8, 4], [3, 4], false),
            (ChordId.Cm9, "Cm9", 2, 6, [4, 4, 3, 1, 8, 4], [2, 3, 4], false),

            (ChordId.C5, "C5", 3, 1, [-11, 5, 4, 15, 15, 13], [1, 4, 5, 6], false),
            (ChordId.D5, "D5", 3, 2, [2, 4, 2, 0, 0, 2], [1, 3, 4, 5, 6], false),
            (ChordId.G5, "G5", 3, 3, [-21, 8, 4, 5, 5, 3], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], true),
            (ChordId.A5, "A5", 3, 4, [5, 5, 2, 2, 0, 1], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], false),
            (ChordId.Cmaj9, "Cmaj9", 3, 5, [0, 3, 4, 5, 7, 13], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], false),
            (ChordId.Cadd9, "Cadd9", 3, 6, [-11, 9, 8, 5, 7, 13], [4, 5], false),

            (ChordId.G_B, "G/B", 4, 1, [3, 0, 0, 0, 2, 3], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], true),
            (ChordId.Am7_G, "Am7/G", 4, 2, [0, 1, 2, 2, 0, 3], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], true),
            (ChordId.D_Fsharp, "D/F#", 4, 3, [2, 4, 2, 0, -3, 2], [1, 3, 4, 5, 6], false),
            (ChordId.F_A, "F/A", 4, 4, [1, 1, 2, 3, 0, 1], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6], true),
            (ChordId.A, "A", 4, 5, [0, 3, 2, 2, 0, 1], [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], false),
            (ChordId.Dm, "Dm", 4, 6, [1, 4, 2, 0, 0, 4], [1, 3, 4, 5], false)
        };

        var table = new Dictionary<ChordId, ChordGroundTruthInfo>(definitions.Length);
        foreach (var definition in definitions)
        {
            var actualMidi = new byte[6];
            for (int i = 0; i < actualMidi.Length; i++)
                actualMidi[i] = (byte)Math.Clamp(OpenStringMidi1To6[i] + definition.Offsets[i], 0, 127);

            table.Add(definition.Id, new ChordGroundTruthInfo(
                definition.Id, definition.Name, definition.Page, definition.Slot,
                definition.Offsets, actualMidi, definition.SafeStrings, definition.FullStrum));
        }

        Table = table;
    }

    public static byte FindExactCleanString(ChordId chord, byte targetMidiPitch, IReadOnlySet<byte>? occupiedStrings = null)
    {
        if (!Table.TryGetValue(chord, out var info)) return 0;
        foreach (byte stringNo in info.SafeCleanStrings)
        {
            if ((occupiedStrings == null || !occupiedStrings.Contains(stringNo)) &&
                info.ActualMidi1To6[stringNo - 1] == targetMidiPitch)
                return stringNo;
        }
        return 0;
    }

    /// <summary>Finds the closest safe string with the same pitch class, or zero when none exists.</summary>
    public static byte FindPitchClassCompatibleString(ChordId chord, byte targetMidiPitch, IReadOnlySet<byte>? occupiedStrings = null)
    {
        byte exact = FindExactCleanString(chord, targetMidiPitch, occupiedStrings);
        if (exact != 0) return exact;
        if (!Table.TryGetValue(chord, out var info)) return 0;

        byte bestString = 0;
        int bestDistance = int.MaxValue;
        foreach (byte stringNo in info.SafeCleanStrings)
        {
            if (occupiedStrings != null && occupiedStrings.Contains(stringNo)) continue;
            byte actualPitch = info.ActualMidi1To6[stringNo - 1];
            if (actualPitch % 12 != targetMidiPitch % 12) continue;
            int distance = Math.Abs(actualPitch - targetMidiPitch);
            if (distance < bestDistance)
            {
                bestString = stringNo;
                bestDistance = distance;
            }
        }
        return bestString;
    }
}

```

## `Guitar/FretConverter.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

/// <summary>
/// 标准六弦吉他调弦与音域配置 (EADGBE)
/// 1弦=E4(64), 2弦=B3(59), 3弦=G3(55), 4弦=D3(50), 5弦=A2(45), 6弦=E2(40)
/// 覆盖 0~24 品: 最低音 6弦0品 E2(40) ~ 最高音 1弦24品 E6(88) 共 49 个全半音
/// </summary>
public sealed class GuitarTuning
{
    public byte[] OpenStrings { get; set; } = new byte[6]
    {
        64, // 1弦 E4 (最高音弦, 游戏键 6)
        59, // 2弦 B3 (游戏键 5)
        55, // 3弦 G3 (游戏键 4)
        50, // 4弦 D3 (游戏键 3)
        45, // 5弦 A2 (游戏键 2)
        40  // 6弦 E2 (最低音弦, 游戏键 1)
    };

    public byte GetOpenMidiNote(int stringNumber) =>
        (stringNumber >= 1 && stringNumber <= 6) ? OpenStrings[stringNumber - 1] : (byte)64;

    public byte MinPlayableNote => OpenStrings[5]; // 6弦0品: 40 (E2)
    public byte MaxPlayableNote => (byte)(OpenStrings[0] + 24); // 1弦24品: 64 + 24 = 88 (E6)

    public static readonly GuitarTuning Standard = new();
}

/// <summary>
/// 琴弦与品格候选位置 (0~24 品与 5 页轮盘槽位映射)
/// </summary>
public readonly struct FretCandidate
{
    public byte StringNo { get; } // 1..6
    public byte Fret { get; }     // 0..24
    public byte Page { get; }     // 1..5 (P5/S1 = 0 品空弦)
    public byte Slot { get; }     // 1..6
    public bool IsSkip { get; }   // 是否为跳音/静音候选

    public FretCandidate(byte stringNo, byte fret, bool isSkip = false)
    {
        StringNo = stringNo;
        Fret = fret;
        IsSkip = isSkip;
        if (isSkip)
        {
            Page = 0;
            Slot = 0;
        }
        else
        {
            var (p, s) = FretConverter.GetPageAndSlot(fret);
            Page = p;
            Slot = s;
        }
    }

    public static FretCandidate Skip => new(1, 0, isSkip: true);

    public override string ToString() => IsSkip ? "SKIP" : $"S{StringNo}-F{Fret}(P{Page}S{Slot})";
}

public sealed class FretConverterOptions
{
    public double PageChangeCost { get; set; } = 6.0;          // 动态换页基准成本 (v4.3 降低至 4~8 范围，避免选怪异高品)
    public double FretDistanceCost { get; set; } = 1.0;        // 换品距离权重
    public double StringDistanceCost { get; set; } = 0.8;      // 换弦距离权重
    public double HandPositionShiftPenalty { get; set; } = 1.2; // 跨大弦距惩罚 (>= 3 弦大跳)
    public double HighFretCost { get; set; } = 0.6;            // 13~18品中等惩罚
    public double ExtremeHighFretCost { get; set; } = 2.0;     // 19~24品较高惩罚
    public double NaturalPositionCost { get; set; } = 2.0;     // 自然弦位偏好成本
    public double SlotChangeCost { get; set; } = 3.0;          // 同页内轮盘换槽成本 (优先在同品不同弦弹奏，避免频繁换槽)
    public double SkipNoteCost { get; set; } = 2000.0;         // 跳音安全兜底成本
    
    // v4.3 快速翻页与轮盘稳定参数
    public long PageKeyHoldUs { get; set; } = 25_000;          // R/T 按键保持时间 25ms (20~35ms)
    public long PageInterTapGapUs { get; set; } = 12_000;      // 连续 R/T 按键间隔 12ms (8~20ms)
    public long PageInputSafetyUs { get; set; } = 8_000;       // 调度抖动安全余量 8ms (5~15ms)
    public long WheelMouseSettleUs { get; set; } = 55_000;     // 轮盘选槽稳定时间 55ms (标准提前量)
    public long SlotSelectDelayUs { get; set; } = 35_000;      // 选槽物理耗时 35ms (移鼠+点击+稳定)
    public byte InitialPage { get; set; } = 1;                 // 初始起始页面 (默认稳定为 P1)
    public bool IsMonophonicSource { get; set; } = false;      // 是否已经是单音识别结果 (若是，跳过 Sanitize)

    // 兼容旧属性
    public long PageSwitchDelayUs
    {
        get => PageKeyHoldUs + PageInterTapGapUs + PageInputSafetyUs;
        set
        {
            if (value > 0)
            {
                PageKeyHoldUs = (long)(value * 0.55);
                PageInterTapGapUs = (long)(value * 0.30);
                PageInputSafetyUs = (long)(value * 0.15);
            }
        }
    }

    public long SlotSettleUs
    {
        get => WheelMouseSettleUs;
        set { if (value > 0) WheelMouseSettleUs = value; }
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// 品格模式转换器: 0~24 品完整映射、整曲统一八度移调与 Viterbi DP 路径规划
/// </summary>
public static class FretConverter
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 根据品格编号计算所属轮盘页面 (1..5) 与扇区槽位 (1..6)
    /// </summary>
    public static (byte Page, byte Slot) GetPageAndSlot(byte fret)
    {
        if (fret == 0)
        {
            return (5, 1); // P5: 0 品空弦
        }

        byte page = (byte)(((fret - 1) / 6) + 1);
        byte slot = (byte)(((fret - 1) % 6) + 1);
        return (page, slot);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 对单音生成所有可用的 [琴弦, 品格] 候选 (优先 1~24 品，0 品 P5 兜底)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<FretCandidate> GenerateCandidates(byte noteNumber, GuitarTuning tuning)
    {
        var list = new List<FretCandidate>(6);
        for (int s = 1; s <= 6; s++)
        {
            int openNote = tuning.GetOpenMidiNote(s);
            int fret = noteNumber - openNote;
            if (fret >= 1 && fret <= 24)
            {
                list.Add(new FretCandidate((byte)s, (byte)fret));
            }
        }

        // 仅在 1~24 品完全无解 (如最低音 40, 即 6弦0品) 时，才加入 0 品作为兜底
        if (list.Count == 0)
        {
            for (int s = 1; s <= 6; s++)
            {
                int openNote = tuning.GetOpenMidiNote(s);
                int fret = noteNumber - openNote;
                if (fret == 0)
                {
                    list.Add(new FretCandidate((byte)s, 0));
                }
            }
        }

        return list;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 吉他品格独奏保护：确保输入序列为纯净主旋律线。单音模式直通；仅在多音输入且真实时间区间重叠时保留主旋律音符。
    /// </summary>
    public static List<RawMidiNote> SanitizeForFretSolo(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes, bool isMonophonic = false)
    {
        if (notes == null || notes.Count <= 1) return notes?.ToList() ?? new List<RawMidiNote>();

        // 过滤打击乐通道
        var nonDrum = notes.Where(n => n.Channel != 9).OrderBy(n => n.StartUs).ToList();
        if (nonDrum.Count <= 1) return nonDrum;

        // 若输入源已经标记为单音识别结果 (ContinuousMelody, TrackPriority 单轨, Monophonic, HighestNote)，完全跳过 Sanitize
        if (isMonophonic)
        {
            return nonDrum;
        }

        // 同步复音去重：当多个音符在相同时刻 (<= 15ms 容差) 并发时，保留最高力度/最高音高作为单音主旋律
        // 起音时间不同的连续音符 (如琶音、颤音、连续单音) 完整保留，绝不因延音跨度而吞噬后续音符
        const long simultaneousToleranceUs = 15_000; // 15ms 和弦并发判定容差
        var result = new List<RawMidiNote>(nonDrum.Count);
        
        int i = 0;
        while (i < nonDrum.Count)
        {
            // Anchor the tolerance window to the first onset in the group. Comparing against
            // `best.StartUs` lets a higher note move the window forward and chain-merge notes
            // whose total onset spread is greater than the simultaneous tolerance.
            long groupStartUs = nonDrum[i].StartUs;
            var best = nonDrum[i];
            var pitchesInGroup = new HashSet<byte> { best.NoteNumber };
            int j = i + 1;

            while (j < nonDrum.Count && (nonDrum[j].StartUs - groupStartUs) < simultaneousToleranceUs)
            {
                // A repeated pitch with a later onset is a retrigger, not another chord tone.
                // Start a new group so fast repeated notes survive even inside the 15ms window.
                if (nonDrum[j].StartUs > groupStartUs && pitchesInGroup.Contains(nonDrum[j].NoteNumber))
                    break;

                // 主旋律提取准则：并发和弦中优先保留高音声部 (主旋律)，相同音高保留最高力度
                if (nonDrum[j].NoteNumber > best.NoteNumber ||
                   (nonDrum[j].NoteNumber == best.NoteNumber && nonDrum[j].Velocity > best.Velocity))
                {
                    best = nonDrum[j];
                }
                pitchesInGroup.Add(nonDrum[j].NoteNumber);
                j++;
            }

            result.Add(best);
            i = j;
        }

        return result;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 将主旋律音符序列转换为最优品格演奏事件 (0~24 品全局 DP)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<FretPlayEvent> Convert(
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> melodyNotes,
        GuitarTuning? tuning = null,
        FretConverterOptions? options = null)
    {
        if (melodyNotes == null || melodyNotes.Count == 0)
            return new List<FretPlayEvent>();

        tuning ??= GuitarTuning.Standard;
        options ??= new FretConverterOptions();

        // 0. 吉他品格独奏保护：确保输入序列为纯净主旋律线，绝不整曲重跑最高音切片
        var cleanNotes = SanitizeForFretSolo(melodyNotes, options.IsMonophonicSource);
        if (cleanNotes.Count == 0) return new List<FretPlayEvent>();

        // 1. 全局最佳统一八度移调 (加权评分，禁止逐音 Clamp)
        int bestOctaveShift = FindBestGlobalOctaveShift(cleanNotes, tuning);

        // 2. 为每个音符生成候选指法，超音域音符生成 Skip 候选
        var candidateGrid = new List<List<FretCandidate>>(cleanNotes.Count);
        var finalNotes = new List<byte>(cleanNotes.Count);

        foreach (var note in cleanNotes)
        {
            int shifted = note.NoteNumber + bestOctaveShift;
            if (shifted >= tuning.MinPlayableNote && shifted <= tuning.MaxPlayableNote)
            {
                byte finalNote = (byte)shifted;
                finalNotes.Add(finalNote);

                var candidates = GenerateCandidates(finalNote, tuning);
                candidates.Add(FretCandidate.Skip); // 兜底候选
                candidateGrid.Add(candidates);
            }
            else
            {
                // 超音域，直接置为 Skip 候选，绝不暴力 Clamp 产生刺耳错音 (方案 5.1)
                finalNotes.Add((byte)Math.Clamp(shifted, 0, 127));
                candidateGrid.Add(new List<FretCandidate> { FretCandidate.Skip });
            }
        }

        // 3. 动态规划寻找全局最优指法路径 (Viterbi DP)
        var (bestPath, conflictFlags) = FingeringDpSolve(candidateGrid, cleanNotes, finalNotes, options);

        // 4. 构建输出事件
        var result = new List<FretPlayEvent>(cleanNotes.Count);
        for (int i = 0; i < cleanNotes.Count; i++)
        {
            var note = cleanNotes[i];
            var chosen = bestPath[i];
            bool isSkipped = chosen.IsSkip;

            result.Add(new FretPlayEvent(
                timeUs: note.StartUs,
                stringNo: chosen.StringNo,
                fret: chosen.Fret,
                noteNumber: finalNotes[i],
                velocity: note.Velocity,
                durationUs: note.DurationUs > 0 ? note.DurationUs : 300_000,
                page: chosen.Page,
                slot: chosen.Slot,
                isTimingConflict: conflictFlags[i],
                isSkipped: isSkipped
            ));
        }

        return result;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 全局最佳八度移调 (方案 5.2: 动态扩展候选八度范围，结合时长/力度/音乐重要度评分)
    /// 采用时长与力度加权评分，禁止对音域外音符暴力 Clamp
    /// </summary>
    public static int FindBestGlobalOctaveShift(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes, GuitarTuning tuning)
    {
        if (notes == null || notes.Count == 0) return 0;

        int minNote = notes.Min(n => n.NoteNumber);
        int maxNote = notes.Max(n => n.NoteNumber);

        // 动态根据音域覆盖生成候选整数八度
        var candidateShifts = new HashSet<int> { 0, -12, 12, -24, 24 };
        for (int s = -48; s <= 48; s += 12)
        {
            if (maxNote + s >= tuning.MinPlayableNote && minNote + s <= tuning.MaxPlayableNote)
            {
                candidateShifts.Add(s);
            }
        }

        int bestShift = 0;
        double maxScore = double.MinValue;

        foreach (int shift in candidateShifts)
        {
            double score = 0;
            foreach (var n in notes)
            {
                int val = n.NoteNumber + shift;
                double durWeight = Math.Clamp((n.DurationUs > 0 ? n.DurationUs : 300_000) / 100_000.0, 0.5, 10.0);
                double velWeight = Math.Clamp(n.Velocity / 127.0, 0.4, 1.0);
                double weight = durWeight * velWeight;

                if (val >= tuning.MinPlayableNote && val <= tuning.MaxPlayableNote)
                {
                    score += 10.0 * weight;
                    // 仅对超过 20 品的极端高把位 (> 1弦20品: 64 + 20 = 84) 施加轻微偏好，绝不惩罚 67~84 之间的自然人声主旋律
                    if (val > 84)
                    {
                        score -= (val - 84) * 0.05 * weight;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    // 超出吉他可奏音域 (40~88)，施加重度不可奏扣分
                    score -= 50.0 * weight;
                }
            }

            // 强烈倾向于原调保持音高真实性 (shift == 0)。
            // 只要原曲在吉他音域内可奏，每偏离 1 个八度施加 240 分大额惩罚，杜绝无故八度下沉
            score -= Math.Abs(shift) * 20.0;

            if (score > maxScore)
            {
                maxScore = score;
                bestShift = shift;
            }
        }

        return bestShift;
    }
```
