```csharp
    public static double GetPositionalCost(FretCandidate cand, FretConverterOptions opt, byte noteNumber = 0, RawMidiNote? sourceNote = null)
    {
        if (cand.IsSkip) return 0;

        double cost = 0;

        // 1. 分段阶梯高品惩罚: 0~12品零成本，13~18品低成本，19~24品高成本
        if (cand.Fret > 18)
        {
            cost += (6 * opt.HighFretCost) + (cand.Fret - 18) * opt.ExtremeHighFretCost;
        }
        else if (cand.Fret > 12)
        {
            cost += (cand.Fret - 12) * opt.HighFretCost;
        }

        // 2. 自然弦位偏好:
        // 高音 (>= 64, E4+) 优先 1/2 弦
        // 中音 (50..63, D3..D#4) 优先 2/3/4 弦
        // 低音 (< 50, < D3) 优先 4/5/6 弦
        if (noteNumber > 0)
        {
            bool isNatural = false;
            if (noteNumber >= 64)
            {
                isNatural = (cand.StringNo <= 2);
            }
            else if (noteNumber >= 50)
            {
                isNatural = (cand.StringNo >= 2 && cand.StringNo <= 4);
            }
            else
            {
                isNatural = (cand.StringNo >= 4);
            }

            if (!isNatural)
            {
                cost += opt.NaturalPositionCost;
            }
        }

        // GTP's original/equivalent fingering is a soft prior. A playable same-page
        // alternative can still win when the source fingering needs an unsafe page switch.
        if (sourceNote?.OrigString == cand.StringNo && sourceNote?.OrigFret == cand.Fret)
            cost -= 4.5;

        return cost;
    }

    private static (List<FretCandidate> Path, List<bool> ConflictFlags) FingeringDpSolve(
        List<List<FretCandidate>> grid,
        IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes,
        IReadOnlyList<byte> finalNotes,
        FretConverterOptions options)
    {
        int n = grid.Count;
        if (n == 0) return (new List<FretCandidate>(), new List<bool>());

        var dp = new double[n][];
        var parent = new int[n][];
        var conflict = new bool[n][];

        // Step 0
        dp[0] = new double[grid[0].Count];
        parent[0] = new int[grid[0].Count];
        conflict[0] = new bool[grid[0].Count];
        bool hasPlayable = grid[0].Any(cand => !cand.IsSkip);

        for (int c = 0; c < grid[0].Count; c++)
        {
            var cand = grid[0][c];
            if (cand.IsSkip)
            {
                // 若首音有可奏候选，首音绝无准备不及时的理由 (PreRoll 提前准备)，禁止跳过首音
                dp[0][c] = hasPlayable ? double.PositiveInfinity : options.SkipNoteCost;
                conflict[0][c] = true;
            }
            else
            {
                // 首音若偏离初始页面，由 PreRoll 提前消化翻页，施加轻微初始偏好
                double initPageCost = Math.Abs(cand.Page - options.InitialPage) * (options.PageChangeCost * 0.2);
                dp[0][c] = initPageCost + GetPositionalCost(cand, options, finalNotes[0], notes[0]);
                conflict[0][c] = false;
            }
            parent[0][c] = -1;
        }

        // Steps 1 to n - 1
        for (int i = 1; i < n; i++)
        {
            var curCandidates = grid[i];
            var prevCandidates = grid[i - 1];
            long deltaUs = notes[i].StartUs - notes[i - 1].StartUs;
            byte curNote = finalNotes[i];

            dp[i] = new double[curCandidates.Count];
            parent[i] = new int[curCandidates.Count];
            conflict[i] = new bool[curCandidates.Count];

            for (int c = 0; c < curCandidates.Count; c++)
            {
                var cur = curCandidates[c];
                double minCost = double.PositiveInfinity;
                int bestPrev = 0;
                bool bestConflict = false;

                for (int p = 0; p < prevCandidates.Count; p++)
                {
                    double prevDp = dp[i - 1][p];
                    if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(prevDp)) continue;

                    var prev = prevCandidates[p];
                    FretCandidate transitionPrevious = prev;
                    long transitionDeltaUs = deltaUs;
                    if (prev.IsSkip && TryFindPreviousPlayedState(grid, parent, i - 1, p, out var lastPlayed, out int lastPlayedIndex))
                    {
                        transitionPrevious = lastPlayed;
                        transitionDeltaUs = notes[i].StartUs - notes[lastPlayedIndex].StartUs;
                    }
                    var (stepCost, isConflict) = CalculateTransitionCost(transitionPrevious, cur, transitionDeltaUs, options, curNote, notes[i]);
                    if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(stepCost)) continue;

                    double totalCost = prevDp + stepCost;
                    if (totalCost < minCost)
                    {
                        minCost = totalCost;
                        bestPrev = p;
                        bestConflict = isConflict;
                    }
                }

                dp[i][c] = minCost;
                parent[i][c] = bestPrev;
                conflict[i][c] = bestConflict;
            }
        }

        // Find minimum at last step
        double finalMin = double.PositiveInfinity;
        int bestLastIdx = 0;
        for (int c = 0; c < grid[n - 1].Count; c++)
        {
            if (dp[n - 1][c] < finalMin)
            {
                finalMin = dp[n - 1][c];
                bestLastIdx = c;
            }
        }

        // 极端兜底: 若末尾全为 +Infinity，选 Skip 节点
        if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(finalMin))
        {
            int skipIdx = grid[n - 1].FindIndex(cand => cand.IsSkip);
            bestLastIdx = skipIdx >= 0 ? skipIdx : 0;
        }

        // Backtrack
        var path = new FretCandidate[n];
        var flags = new bool[n];
        int currIdx = bestLastIdx;
        for (int i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        {
            path[i] = grid[i][currIdx];
            flags[i] = conflict[i][currIdx] || path[i].IsSkip;
            currIdx = parent[i][currIdx];
            if (currIdx < 0 && i > 0)
            {
                currIdx = 0;
            }
        }

        return (path.ToList(), flags.ToList());
    }

    private static bool TryFindPreviousPlayedState(
        IReadOnlyList<List<FretCandidate>> grid,
        IReadOnlyList<int[]> parents,
        int stageIndex,
        int stateIndex,
        out FretCandidate lastPlayed,
        out int lastPlayedIndex)
    {
        for (int index = stageIndex; index >= 0; index--)
        {
            if (stateIndex < 0 || stateIndex >= grid[index].Count) break;
            FretCandidate candidate = grid[index][stateIndex];
            if (!candidate.IsSkip)
            {
                lastPlayed = candidate;
                lastPlayedIndex = index;
                return true;
            }
            stateIndex = parents[index][stateIndex];
        }
        lastPlayed = FretCandidate.Skip;
        lastPlayedIndex = -1;
        return false;
    }

    public static (double Cost, bool IsTimingConflict) CalculateTransitionCost(
        FretCandidate prev,
        FretCandidate cur,
        long deltaUs,
        FretConverterOptions opt,
        byte curNoteNumber = 0,
        RawMidiNote? sourceNote = null)
    {
        if (cur.IsSkip)
        {
            return (opt.SkipNoteCost, true);
        }

        if (prev.IsSkip)
        {
            return (GetPositionalCost(cur, opt, curNoteNumber, sourceNote), false);
        }

        int pageDiff = Math.Abs(cur.Page - prev.Page);
        bool fretChanged = (cur.Fret != prev.Fret);
        bool slotChanged = (cur.Slot != prev.Slot);

        // 实际物理准备耗时 (v4.3 快速翻页串行模型: 串行 TapHold + InterTapGap + MouseSettle + Safety)
        long singleTapUs = opt.PageKeyHoldUs + opt.PageInterTapGapUs;
        long requiredPrepareUs = 0;
        if (pageDiff > 0)
        {
            requiredPrepareUs = pageDiff * singleTapUs + (slotChanged ? opt.WheelMouseSettleUs : 0) + opt.PageInputSafetyUs;
        }
        else if (slotChanged || fretChanged)
        {
            requiredPrepareUs = opt.WheelMouseSettleUs + opt.PageInputSafetyUs;
        }
        else
        {
            requiredPrepareUs = 0; // 同页同品槽: 0ms
        }

        // 核心硬约束: 当物理准备时间不足时，直接返回 +Infinity，配合 SkipNote 兜底，绝不在旧品位发音
        if (deltaUs > 0 && deltaUs < requiredPrepareUs)
        {
            return (double.PositiveInfinity, true);
        }

        // 动态换页代价 (与实测控制耗时挂钩，避免迫使 DP 选怪品)
        double dynamicPageCost = opt.PageChangeCost * (singleTapUs / 37000.0);
        double cost = 0;
        cost += pageDiff * dynamicPageCost;
        if (pageDiff == 0 && (slotChanged || fretChanged))
        {
            cost += opt.SlotChangeCost;
        }

        // 快速连奏品格锁定: 当音符间隔小于 320ms 时 (密集快速连奏)，频繁切品切槽极易导致鼠标抢跑或漏音
        // 间隔越短，施加平滑加权惩罚，驱动 DP 优先选用同品不同弦演奏 (按键 0 延迟且绝无鼠标竞态)
        if (deltaUs > 0 && deltaUs < 320_000 && (slotChanged || fretChanged || pageDiff > 0))
        {
            double rapidFactor = (320_000 - deltaUs) / 320_000.0;
            cost += rapidFactor * 6.0;
        }

        cost += Math.Abs(cur.Fret - prev.Fret) * opt.FretDistanceCost;
        cost += Math.Abs(cur.StringNo - prev.StringNo) * opt.StringDistanceCost;
        if (Math.Abs(cur.StringNo - prev.StringNo) >= 3)
        {
            cost += opt.HandPositionShiftPenalty;
        }
        double positionalCost = GetPositionalCost(cur, opt, curNoteNumber, sourceNote);
        if (deltaUs > 0 && deltaUs < 250_000 && pageDiff == 0 && cur.Fret is >= 13 and <= 18)
        {
            // A rapid same-page equivalent fingering is preferable to a page
            // change. Temporarily remove its ordinary 13..18 fret surcharge.
            positionalCost -= (cur.Fret - 12) * opt.HighFretCost;
        }
        cost += positionalCost;

        return (cost, false);
    }
}

```

## `Guitar/GuitarCompiler.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

public enum GuitarActionType : byte
{
    PageNav = 1,     // 物理按键 R / T 翻页 (0x13 / 0x14)
    WheelSelect = 2, // 点击轮盘指定 Slot (1..6)
    StringDown = 3,  // 琴弦数字键按下 (1..6)
    StringUp = 4,    // 琴弦数字键抬起 (1..6)
    StrumClick = 5,  // 安全点扫弦点击 (左键/右键)
    PageNavUp = 6,   // 定时抬起翻页键，不阻塞播放线程
    ModeSwitchDown = 7, // Z: 切换品格/和弦模式，游戏页面归位 P1
    ModeSwitchUp = 8
}

public readonly struct GuitarAction
{
    public long TimeUs { get; }
    public GuitarActionType Type { get; }
    public byte Value { get; }          // SlotIndex (1..6) 或 StringNo (1..6) 或 Page
    public ushort ScanCode { get; }     // 物理按键 ScanCode (0x13=R, 0x14=T, 0x02..0x07=1..6)
    public MouseButton MouseButton { get; } // 鼠标按键
    public long DeadlineUs { get; }     // 截止时间戳 (超过则不可在旧状态执行)
    public byte RequiredPage { get; }   // 依赖的游戏页面
    public byte RequiredSlot { get; }   // 依赖的轮盘槽位
    public GameActionResource Resource { get; }
    public FallbackPolicy FallbackPolicy { get; }
    public byte NoteNumber { get; }

    public GuitarAction(
        long timeUs,
        GuitarActionType type,
        byte value,
        ushort scanCode = 0,
        MouseButton mouseButton = MouseButton.Left,
        long deadlineUs = 0,
        byte requiredPage = 0,
        byte requiredSlot = 0,
        GameActionResource resource = GameActionResource.None,
        FallbackPolicy fallbackPolicy = FallbackPolicy.Skip,
        byte noteNumber = 0)
    {
        TimeUs = timeUs;
        Type = type;
        Value = value;
        ScanCode = scanCode;
        MouseButton = mouseButton;
        DeadlineUs = (deadlineUs > 0) ? deadlineUs : (timeUs + 60_000);
        RequiredPage = requiredPage;
        RequiredSlot = requiredSlot;
        Resource = resource;
        FallbackPolicy = fallbackPolicy;
        NoteNumber = noteNumber;
    }

    public override string ToString() => $"{TimeUs} us: {Type} Value={Value} Scan=0x{ScanCode:X2} Mouse={MouseButton} Req=P{RequiredPage}S{RequiredSlot}";
}

public sealed class GuitarCompilerOptions
{
    // v4.3 快速翻页参数 (方案 4.1)
    public long PageKeyHoldUs { get; set; } = 25_000;       // R/T 按键保持时间 25ms (20~35ms)
    public long PageInterTapGapUs { get; set; } = 12_000;   // 连续两个 R/T Tap 的最小间隔 12ms (8~20ms)
    public long PageInputSafetyUs { get; set; } = 8_000;    // 调度抖动安全余量 8ms (5~15ms)
    public long WheelMouseSettleUs { get; set; } = 55_000;  // 轮盘选槽稳定时间 55ms (标准提前量)
    public long StringTapDurationUs { get; set; } = 25_000; // 琴弦脉冲按下时长 25ms，防长按卡键
    public long MinRetriggerGapUs { get; set; } = 25_000;   // 同弦过密防抖 25ms
    public long ReleaseGapUs { get; set; } = 22_000;        // 跨帧抬键间隙 22ms
    public long MinHoldUs { get; set; } = 20_000;           // 游戏识别要求至少 20ms
    public long MaxSustainUs { get; set; } = 1_500_000;     // 最大长按延音 1.5s
    public long CloseNotesThresholdUs { get; set; } = 280_000;
    public bool EnableSustain { get; set; } = true;         // 默认启用智能动态延音 (两键过近点按防吞音，尾音/空隙充分延音)
    public GuitarPlayStyle PlayStyle { get; set; } = GuitarPlayStyle.Hybrid; // 演奏风格策略
    public AutoStrumMode AutoStrum { get; set; } = AutoStrumMode.Auto;
    public bool EnableInitialSync { get; set; } = false;    // 是否强制在开头排布 4xR / 3xR 同步动作
    public byte InitialPage { get; set; } = 1;              // 初始所在页码 (默认稳定为 P1)
    public ChordVoicingProfile VoicingProfile { get; set; } = new();

    // 兼容旧属性
    public long PageSwitchDelayUs
    {
        get => PageKeyHoldUs + PageInterTapGapUs + PageInputSafetyUs;
        set
        {
            if (value > 0)
            {
                PageKeyHoldUs = (long)(value * 0.55);
                PageInterTapGapUs = (long)(value * 0.30);
                PageInputSafetyUs = (long)(value * 0.15);
            }
        }
    }

    public long SlotSettleUs
    {
        get => WheelMouseSettleUs;
        set { if (value > 0) WheelMouseSettleUs = value; }
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// 吉他动作编译器: 负责翻页预加载时序、轮盘选品、发音按键与扫弦动作生成
/// </summary>
public static class GuitarCompiler
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 编译品格演奏事件为动作序列 (支持 0~24 品与 R/T 翻页预加载)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<GuitarAction> CompileFretEvents(
        IReadOnlyList<FretPlayEvent> fretEvents,
        WheelPreset preset,
        GuitarCompilerOptions? options = null)
    {
        if (fretEvents == null || fretEvents.Count == 0)
            return new List<GuitarAction>();

        options ??= new GuitarCompilerOptions();
        NormalizeMinimumTiming(options);
        var actions = new List<GuitarAction>();
        var barrierTimes = new HashSet<long>();

        // 1. 生成轮盘翻页与槽位选择准备动作 (提前调度，允许负时间戳用于 PreRoll 预热，消除 0ms 爆发拥堵)
        byte currentPage = options.InitialPage; // 起始页码 (默认稳定为 P1)
        byte currentSlot = 0;
        long lastNoteTimeUs = -1;
        long lastControlFinishedUs = long.MinValue / 2;
        long navHoldUs = Math.Max(25_000, options.PageKeyHoldUs);
        long navGapUs = Math.Max(15_000, options.PageInterTapGapUs);
        long stepDelayUs = navHoldUs + navGapUs;
        long wheelSettleUs = Math.Max(55_000, options.WheelMouseSettleUs);
        long minHoldUs = Math.Max(20_000, options.MinHoldUs);

        foreach (var ev in fretEvents)
        {
            if (ev.IsSkipped) continue;

            byte targetPage = ev.Page;
            byte targetSlot = ev.Slot > 0 ? ev.Slot : (byte)1;
            if (ev.Page == 0 || ev.Slot == 0)
            {
                (targetPage, targetSlot) = FretConverter.GetPageAndSlot(ev.Fret);
            }

            if (targetPage != currentPage || targetSlot != currentSlot)
            {
                int pageDiff = targetPage - currentPage;
                int steps = Math.Abs(pageDiff);
                long idealControlStartUs = ev.TimeUs - wheelSettleUs - steps * stepDelayUs;
                long earliestSafeStartUs = lastNoteTimeUs >= 0
                    ? lastNoteTimeUs + Math.Max(minHoldUs, options.StringTapDurationUs) + 6_000
                    : long.MinValue / 2;
                if (lastControlFinishedUs > long.MinValue / 4)
                    earliestSafeStartUs = Math.Max(earliestSafeStartUs, lastControlFinishedUs + 6_000);
                long cursorUs = Math.Max(idealControlStartUs, earliestSafeStartUs);

                if (steps > 0)
                {
                    ushort navScan = (pageDiff > 0) ? preset.PageNextScanCode : preset.PagePrevScanCode; // T 或 R
                    int direction = Math.Sign(pageDiff);
                    for (int k = 0; k < steps; k++)
                    {
                        long navTime = cursorUs;
                        byte navPage = (byte)(currentPage + direction * (k + 1));
                        actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                            navTime,
                            GuitarActionType.PageNav,
                            navPage,
                            navScan,
                            MouseButton.Left,
                            deadlineUs: navTime + 500_000,
                            requiredPage: targetPage,
                            requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                            resource: GameActionResource.Nav));
                        actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                            navTime + navHoldUs,
                            GuitarActionType.PageNavUp,
                            navPage,
                            navScan,
                            MouseButton.Left,
                            deadlineUs: navTime + navHoldUs + 500_000,
                            requiredPage: targetPage,
                            requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                            resource: GameActionResource.Nav));
                        barrierTimes.Add(navTime);
                        cursorUs += stepDelayUs;
                    }
                    currentPage = targetPage;
                }

                long selectTimeUs = cursorUs;
                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                    selectTimeUs,
                    GuitarActionType.WheelSelect,
                    targetSlot,
                    0,
                    MouseButton.Left,
                    deadlineUs: selectTimeUs + 500_000,
                    requiredPage: targetPage,
                    requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                    resource: GameActionResource.Wheel));
                barrierTimes.Add(selectTimeUs);
                currentSlot = targetSlot;
                lastControlFinishedUs = selectTimeUs + wheelSettleUs;
                if (lastControlFinishedUs > ev.TimeUs)
                    ev.TimeUs = lastControlFinishedUs;
            }
            else if (lastControlFinishedUs > ev.TimeUs)
                ev.TimeUs = lastControlFinishedUs;

            lastNoteTimeUs = ev.TimeUs;
        }

        // 2. 按琴弦分组生成弦按键发音动作 (1~6 弦，过滤 IsSkipped 音符)
        var stringTriggers = new Dictionary<byte, List<(long TimeUs, long DurationUs, byte Page, byte Slot, byte NoteNumber)>>();
        foreach (var ev in fretEvents)
        {
            if (ev.IsSkipped) continue;
            if (ev.StringNo < 1 || ev.StringNo > 6) continue;

            if (!stringTriggers.TryGetValue(ev.StringNo, out var list))
            {
                list = new List<(long TimeUs, long DurationUs, byte Page, byte Slot, byte NoteNumber)>();
                stringTriggers[ev.StringNo] = list;
            }
            var (resolvedPage, resolvedSlot) = ev.Page == 0 || ev.Slot == 0
                ? FretConverter.GetPageAndSlot(ev.Fret)
                : (ev.Page, ev.Slot);
            list.Add((ev.TimeUs, ev.DurationUs, resolvedPage, resolvedSlot, ev.NoteNumber));
        }

        // 收集未被跳过音符的全局不重复起音时刻，用于检测整曲相邻音符间隙
        var uniqueFretMoments = fretEvents.Where(e => !e.IsSkipped)
                                          .Select(e => e.TimeUs)
                                          .Distinct()
                                          .OrderBy(t => t)
                                          .ToList();

        foreach (var kvp in stringTriggers)
        {
            byte stringNo = kvp.Key;
            ushort scanCode = (preset.StringScanCodes != null && preset.StringScanCodes.Length >= stringNo)
                ? preset.StringScanCodes[stringNo - 1]
                : GameScanCodes.ForGuitarString(stringNo);

            var items = kvp.Value;
            items.Sort((a, b) => a.TimeUs.CompareTo(b.TimeUs));

            var filtered = new List<(long TimeUs, long DurationUs, byte Page, byte Slot, byte NoteNumber)>(items.Count);
            foreach (var it in items)
            {
                if (filtered.Count == 0 || (it.TimeUs - filtered[^1].TimeUs) >= options.MinRetriggerGapUs)
                {
                    filtered.Add(it);
                }
            }

            long lastUpTime = long.MinValue / 2;
            for (int i = 0; i < filtered.Count; i++)
            {
                long sourceTime = filtered[i].TimeUs;
                long downTime = Math.Max(sourceTime, lastUpTime + options.ReleaseGapUs);
                long rawDuration = filtered[i].DurationUs;
                bool isLastOnString = (i + 1 == filtered.Count);
                long nextDownTime = isLastOnString ? long.MaxValue : filtered[i + 1].TimeUs;

                int momentIdx = uniqueFretMoments.BinarySearch(sourceTime);
                bool isLastMomentInSong = (momentIdx >= 0 && momentIdx == uniqueFretMoments.Count - 1);
                long nextGlobalTime = (!isLastMomentInSong && momentIdx >= 0 && momentIdx + 1 < uniqueFretMoments.Count)
                    ? uniqueFretMoments[momentIdx + 1]
                    : long.MaxValue;

                long holdUs = CalculateDynamicHoldUs(downTime, rawDuration, isLastOnString, nextDownTime, options, nextGlobalTime);
                long upTime = downTime + holdUs;

                // 核心修复 1: 延音释放边界保证 (upTime 绝不可跨越或碰撞 nextDownTime - ReleaseGapUs)
                if (!isLastOnString)
                {
                    long maxAllowedUp = nextDownTime - options.ReleaseGapUs;
                    if (upTime > maxAllowedUp)
                    {
                        upTime = maxAllowedUp;
                        if (upTime - downTime < Math.Max(5_000, options.MinHoldUs))
                        {
                            upTime = downTime + Math.Max(5_000, options.MinHoldUs);
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 核心修复 2: 控制屏障 (Control Barrier)
                // 若在延音期间存在翻页或选槽，在动作发生前提前 6ms 释放琴弦。
                foreach (var bTime in barrierTimes)
                {
                    if (bTime > downTime && bTime < upTime)
                    {
                        long safeUp = bTime - 6_000;
                        if (safeUp >= downTime + Math.Max(5_000, options.MinHoldUs))
                        {
                            upTime = safeUp;
                        }
                    }
                }

                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                    downTime,
                    GuitarActionType.StringDown,
                    stringNo,
                    scanCode,
                    MouseButton.Left,
                    deadlineUs: downTime + 30_000,
                    requiredPage: filtered[i].Page,
                    requiredSlot: filtered[i].Slot,
                    resource: GameActionResource.StringKeys,
                    fallbackPolicy: FallbackPolicy.Skip,
                    noteNumber: filtered[i].NoteNumber));

                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                    upTime,
                    GuitarActionType.StringUp,
                    stringNo,
                    scanCode,
                    MouseButton.Left,
                    deadlineUs: upTime + 60_000,
                    requiredPage: filtered[i].Page,
                    requiredSlot: filtered[i].Slot,
                    resource: GameActionResource.StringKeys));
                lastUpTime = upTime;
            }
        }

        // 3. 排序 (相同时间优先 Up -> PageNav -> WheelSelect -> StrumClick -> Down)
        actions.Sort((a, b) =>
        {
            int cmp = a.TimeUs.CompareTo(b.TimeUs);
            if (cmp != 0) return cmp;
            return GetActionPriority(a.Type).CompareTo(GetActionPriority(b.Type));
        });

        return actions;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 编译和弦与扫弦事件为动作序列 (兼容旧版重载)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<GuitarAction> CompileChordEvents(
        IReadOnlyList<ChordChangeEvent> chordEvents,
        IReadOnlyList<StrumEvent> strumEvents,
        WheelPreset preset,
        GuitarCompilerOptions? options = null)
        => CompileChordEvents(chordEvents, strumEvents, pluckEvents: null, preset, options);

    /// <summary>
    /// 编译和弦、扫弦与按键拨弦事件为动作序列 (支持 4 页 24 和弦与串行 R/T 快速翻页)
    /// </summary>
    public static List<GuitarAction> CompileChordEvents(
        IReadOnlyList<ChordChangeEvent> chordEvents,
        IReadOnlyList<StrumEvent> strumEvents,
        IReadOnlyList<ChordPluckEvent>? pluckEvents,
        WheelPreset preset,
        GuitarCompilerOptions? options = null)
    {
        options ??= new GuitarCompilerOptions();
        NormalizeMinimumTiming(options);
        var actions = new List<GuitarAction>();
        // 1. 和弦轮盘翻页与切换动作 (串行提前准备)
        byte currentPage = options.InitialPage > 0 ? options.InitialPage : (byte)1; // 和弦初始在指定起始页 (默认 P1)
        ChordId currentChord = ChordId.Unknown;

        if (chordEvents != null)
        {
            foreach (var ch in chordEvents)
            {
                if (ch.Chord != currentChord && ch.Chord != ChordId.Unknown)
                {
                    byte targetPage = (ch.Page > 0) ? ch.Page : (ChordAnalyzer.GetDefinition(ch.Chord)?.Page ?? 1);
                    byte targetSlot = (ch.Slot > 0) ? ch.Slot : (ChordAnalyzer.GetDefinition(ch.Chord)?.Slot ?? 1);

                    long selectTimeUs = ch.TimeUs - options.WheelMouseSettleUs;
                    int pageDiff = targetPage - currentPage;
                    int steps = Math.Abs(pageDiff);

                    if (steps > 0)
                    {
                        ushort navScan = (pageDiff > 0) ? preset.PageNextScanCode : preset.PagePrevScanCode; // T 或 R
                        long stepDelayUs = options.PageSwitchDelayUs;
                        int direction = Math.Sign(pageDiff);
                        for (int k = 0; k < steps; k++)
                        {
                            long navTime = selectTimeUs - (steps - k) * stepDelayUs;
                            byte navPage = (byte)(currentPage + direction * (k + 1));
                            actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                                navTime,
                                GuitarActionType.PageNav,
                                navPage,
                                navScan,
                                MouseButton.Left,
                                deadlineUs: selectTimeUs,
                                requiredPage: targetPage,
                                requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                                resource: GameActionResource.Nav));
                            actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                                navTime + options.PageKeyHoldUs,
                                GuitarActionType.PageNavUp,
                                navPage,
                                navScan,
                                MouseButton.Left,
                                deadlineUs: selectTimeUs,
                                requiredPage: targetPage,
                                requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                                resource: GameActionResource.Nav));
                        }
                        currentPage = targetPage;
                    }

                    actions.Add(new GuitarAction(
                        selectTimeUs,
                        GuitarActionType.WheelSelect,
                        targetSlot,
                        0,
                        MouseButton.Left,
                        deadlineUs: ch.TimeUs,
                        requiredPage: targetPage,
                        requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                        resource: GameActionResource.Wheel));
                    currentChord = ch.Chord;
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. 扫弦动作
        var strumList = new List<StrumEvent>();
        if (options.PlayStyle != GuitarPlayStyle.PluckOnly &&
            options.AutoStrum != AutoStrumMode.Disabled &&
            strumEvents != null)
        {
            bool alternateToggle = false;

            foreach (var st in strumEvents)
            {
                MouseButton btn;

                switch (options.AutoStrum)
                {
                    case AutoStrumMode.AllDown:
                        btn = preset.DownStrumButton;
                        break;

                    case AutoStrumMode.Alternate:
                        btn = alternateToggle ? preset.UpStrumButton : preset.DownStrumButton;
                        alternateToggle = !alternateToggle;
                        break;

                    case AutoStrumMode.FollowMidi:
                        btn = (st.Hint == StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow)
                            ? preset.UpStrumButton
                            : preset.DownStrumButton;
                        break;

                    case AutoStrumMode.Auto:
                    default:
                        if (st.Hint == StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh)
                            btn = preset.DownStrumButton;
                        else if (st.Hint == StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow)
                            btn = preset.UpStrumButton;
                        else
                        {
                            btn = alternateToggle ? preset.UpStrumButton : preset.DownStrumButton;
                            alternateToggle = !alternateToggle;
                        }
                        break;
                }

                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(st.TimeUs, GuitarActionType.StrumClick, 0, 0, btn));
                strumList.Add(st);
            }
        }

        // 3. 琴弦按键拨弦动作
        if (options.PlayStyle != GuitarPlayStyle.StrumOnly && pluckEvents != null && pluckEvents.Count > 0)
        {
            List<ChordPluckEvent> plucksToEmit;

            if (options.PlayStyle == GuitarPlayStyle.PluckOnly || strumList.Count == 0)
            {
                plucksToEmit = pluckEvents.ToList();
            }
            else
            {
                // 智能弹拨: 排除扫弦窗口内的音符，保留独立单音与主旋律
                plucksToEmit = pluckEvents.Where(p =>
                {
                    bool coveredByStrum = strumList.Any(st =>
                        p.TimeUs >= (st.TimeUs - 10_000) && p.TimeUs <= (st.TimeUs + 45_000));
                    return !coveredByStrum;
                }).ToList();
            }

            CompilePluckStringActions(plucksToEmit, preset, options, actions);
        }

        actions.Sort((a, b) =>
        {
            int cmp = a.TimeUs.CompareTo(b.TimeUs);
            if (cmp != 0) return cmp;
            return GetActionPriority(a.Type).CompareTo(GetActionPriority(b.Type));
        });

        return actions;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 纯和弦模式动作编译器：使用游戏真实和弦音与安全弦表生成无毒音动作。
    /// </summary>
    public static List<GuitarAction> CompileChordDetailedEvents(
        IReadOnlyList<ChordEvent> chordEvents,
        WheelPreset preset,
        GuitarCompilerOptions? options = null)
    {
        if (chordEvents == null || chordEvents.Count == 0)
            return new List<GuitarAction>();

        options ??= new GuitarCompilerOptions();
        NormalizeMinimumTiming(options);
        var actions = new List<GuitarAction>();
        var lastStringUp = new Dictionary<byte, long>();
        var lastStringDown = new Dictionary<byte, long>();
        long lastControlFinishedUs = long.MinValue / 2;
        long lastAttackFinishedUs = long.MinValue / 2;
        byte currentPage = options.InitialPage is >= 1 and <= 4 ? options.InitialPage : (byte)1;
        byte currentSlot = 0;
        ChordId currentChord = ChordId.Unknown;
        bool alternateStrum = false;

        // Chord string attacks need at least 20 ms for the game to register them.
        long minHoldUs = Math.Max(20_000, options.MinHoldUs);
        long tapUs = Math.Max(minHoldUs, options.StringTapDurationUs);
        long navHoldUs = Math.Max(25_000, options.PageKeyHoldUs);
        long navGapUs = Math.Max(15_000, options.PageInterTapGapUs);
        long navStepUs = navHoldUs + navGapUs;
        long wheelSettleUs = Math.Max(55_000, options.WheelMouseSettleUs);
        const long controlGraceUs = 500_000;

        foreach (var chordEvent in chordEvents.OrderBy(e => e.StartUs))
        {
            if (chordEvent.Chord == ChordId.Unknown ||
                !GameChordGroundTruth.Table.TryGetValue(chordEvent.Chord, out var truth))
                continue;

            byte targetPage = truth.Page;
            byte targetSlot = truth.Slot;
            long attackTimeUs = chordEvent.StartUs;
            bool needsSelection = currentChord != truth.Id || currentPage != targetPage || currentSlot != targetSlot;

            if (needsSelection)
            {
                int pageSteps = Math.Abs(targetPage - currentPage);
                long navDurationUs = pageSteps * navStepUs;
                long idealControlStartUs = attackTimeUs - wheelSettleUs - navDurationUs;
                long earliestControlStartUs = Math.Max(
                    lastControlFinishedUs + 6_000,
                    lastAttackFinishedUs > long.MinValue / 4 ? lastAttackFinishedUs + 6_000 : long.MinValue / 2);
                long controlStartUs = Math.Max(idealControlStartUs, earliestControlStartUs);
                long selectUs = controlStartUs + navDurationUs;
                long readyUs = selectUs + wheelSettleUs;
                if (readyUs > attackTimeUs) attackTimeUs = readyUs;
                if (attackTimeUs - chordEvent.StartUs > controlGraceUs) continue;

                int pageDiff = targetPage - currentPage;
                int direction = Math.Sign(pageDiff);
                ushort navScan = pageDiff >= 0 ? preset.PageNextScanCode : preset.PagePrevScanCode;
                long cursorUs = controlStartUs;
                for (int step = 0; step < pageSteps; step++)
                {
                    byte navPage = (byte)(currentPage + direction * (step + 1));
                    actions.Add(new GuitarAction(cursorUs, GuitarActionType.PageNav, navPage, navScan,
                        deadlineUs: cursorUs + controlGraceUs, requiredPage: targetPage, requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                        resource: GameActionResource.Nav));
                    actions.Add(new GuitarAction(cursorUs + navHoldUs, GuitarActionType.PageNavUp, navPage, navScan,
                        deadlineUs: cursorUs + navHoldUs + controlGraceUs, requiredPage: targetPage, requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                        resource: GameActionResource.Nav));
                    cursorUs += navStepUs;
                }
                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(selectUs, GuitarActionType.WheelSelect, targetSlot,
                    deadlineUs: selectUs + controlGraceUs, requiredPage: targetPage, requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                    resource: GameActionResource.Wheel));
                currentPage = targetPage;
                currentSlot = targetSlot;
                currentChord = truth.Id;
                lastControlFinishedUs = readyUs;
            }

            // A delayed chord selection must also delay later attacks on the same chord.
            if (lastControlFinishedUs > attackTimeUs)
                attackTimeUs = lastControlFinishedUs;
            if (attackTimeUs - chordEvent.StartUs > controlGraceUs) continue;

            ChordArticulation articulation = chordEvent.Articulation;
            if (options.PlayStyle == GuitarPlayStyle.BlockOnly) articulation = ChordArticulation.Block;
            else if (options.PlayStyle == GuitarPlayStyle.StrumOnly) articulation = ChordArticulation.Strum;
            else if (options.PlayStyle == GuitarPlayStyle.PluckOnly) articulation = ChordArticulation.Arpeggio;

            if (articulation == ChordArticulation.Strum && truth.CanUseFullMouseStrum &&
                options.AutoStrum != AutoStrumMode.Disabled)
            {
                MouseButton button = ResolveChordStrumButton(chordEvent.Direction, options.AutoStrum,
                    preset, ref alternateStrum);
                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(attackTimeUs, GuitarActionType.StrumClick, 0, 0, button,
                    deadlineUs: attackTimeUs + 60_000, requiredPage: targetPage, requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                    resource: GameActionResource.Mouse));
                lastAttackFinishedUs = Math.Max(lastAttackFinishedUs, attackTimeUs + 30_000);
                continue;
            }

            var sourceNotes = chordEvent.AttackNotes is { Count: > 0 }
                ? chordEvent.AttackNotes : chordEvent.SourceNotes;
            var uniqueSourceNotes = DeduplicateChordSourceNotes(sourceNotes);
            var targets = new List<(byte StringNo, byte NoteNumber, long TimeUs)>();
            if (articulation is ChordArticulation.Block or ChordArticulation.Strum)
            {
                var occupied = new HashSet<byte>();
                if (uniqueSourceNotes.Count > 0)
                {
                    var orderedNotes = articulation == ChordArticulation.Strum
                        ? uniqueSourceNotes.OrderBy(note => note.StartUs)
                        : uniqueSourceNotes.OrderByDescending(note => note.NoteNumber);
                    foreach (var note in orderedNotes)
                    {
                        byte stringNo = note.OrigString is >= 1 and <= 6 &&
                            truth.SafeCleanStrings.Contains((byte)note.OrigString) &&
                            !occupied.Contains((byte)note.OrigString)
                                ? (byte)note.OrigString
                                : FindSafeChordString(truth.Id, note.NoteNumber, occupied);
                        if (stringNo == 0)
                            stringNo = options.VoicingProfile.MatchNoteToString(note.NoteNumber, truth.Id, occupied, out _);
                        if (occupied.Contains(stringNo)) continue;
                        if (stringNo == 0) continue;
                        occupied.Add(stringNo);
                        targets.Add((stringNo, truth.ActualMidi1To6[stringNo - 1], attackTimeUs));
                    }
                }
                int minTargets = articulation == ChordArticulation.Strum
                    ? Math.Min(3, truth.SafeCleanStrings.Length)
                    : 1;
                if (targets.Count < minTargets)
                {
                    targets.Clear();
                    var fallbackStrings = articulation == ChordArticulation.Strum
                        ? chordEvent.Direction == StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow
                            ? truth.SafeCleanStrings.Order()
                            : truth.SafeCleanStrings.OrderDescending()
                        : truth.SafeCleanStrings.Take(3);
                    int index = 0;
                    foreach (byte stringNo in fallbackStrings.Take(articulation == ChordArticulation.Strum ? 4 : 3))
                    {
                        long staggerUs = articulation == ChordArticulation.Strum ? index * 8_000L : 0;
                        targets.Add((stringNo, truth.ActualMidi1To6[stringNo - 1], attackTimeUs + staggerUs));
                        index++;
                    }
                }
            }
            else if (uniqueSourceNotes.Count > 0)
            {
                var orderedNotes = uniqueSourceNotes.OrderBy(n => n.StartUs).ThenByDescending(n => n.NoteNumber).ToList();
                var occupied = new HashSet<byte>();
                long previousNoteTimeUs = long.MinValue;
                foreach (var note in orderedNotes)
                {
                    if (previousNoteTimeUs != long.MinValue && note.StartUs - previousNoteTimeUs > 15_000)
                        occupied.Clear();
                    byte stringNo = note.OrigString is >= 1 and <= 6 &&
                        truth.SafeCleanStrings.Contains((byte)note.OrigString) &&
                        !occupied.Contains((byte)note.OrigString)
                            ? (byte)note.OrigString
                            : FindSafeChordString(truth.Id, note.NoteNumber, occupied);
                    if (stringNo == 0)
                        stringNo = options.VoicingProfile.MatchNoteToString(note.NoteNumber, truth.Id, occupied, out _);
                    if (occupied.Contains(stringNo)) continue;
                    if (stringNo == 0) continue;
                    occupied.Add(stringNo);
                    targets.Add((stringNo, truth.ActualMidi1To6[stringNo - 1], Math.Max(attackTimeUs, note.StartUs)));
                    previousNoteTimeUs = note.StartUs;
                }
            }

            foreach (var target in targets)
            {
                byte stringNo = target.StringNo;
                if (stringNo is < 1 or > 6) continue;
                long releaseGapUs = Math.Max(22_000, options.ReleaseGapUs);
                long previousDownUs = lastStringDown.GetValueOrDefault(stringNo, long.MinValue / 2);
                long downUs = Math.Max(target.TimeUs, previousDownUs + minHoldUs + releaseGapUs);
                long previousUpUs = lastStringUp.GetValueOrDefault(stringNo, long.MinValue / 2);
                if (previousUpUs > downUs - releaseGapUs)
                {
                    long safeUpUs = Math.Max(previousDownUs + minHoldUs, downUs - releaseGapUs);
                    int previousUpIndex = actions.FindLastIndex(action =>
                        action.Type == GuitarActionType.StringUp && action.Value == stringNo);
                    if (previousUpIndex >= 0 && safeUpUs < previousUpUs)
                    {
                        var previousUp = actions[previousUpIndex];
                        actions[previousUpIndex] = new GuitarAction(safeUpUs, previousUp.Type, previousUp.Value,
                            previousUp.ScanCode, previousUp.MouseButton, safeUpUs + controlGraceUs,
                            previousUp.RequiredPage, previousUp.RequiredSlot, previousUp.Resource,
                            previousUp.FallbackPolicy, previousUp.NoteNumber);
                        lastStringUp[stringNo] = safeUpUs;
                    }
                }
                downUs = Math.Max(downUs, lastStringUp.GetValueOrDefault(stringNo, long.MinValue / 2) + releaseGapUs);
                long upUs = downUs + tapUs;
                ushort scanCode = preset.StringScanCodes != null && preset.StringScanCodes.Length >= stringNo
                    ? preset.StringScanCodes[stringNo - 1] : GameScanCodes.ForGuitarString(stringNo);
                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(downUs, GuitarActionType.StringDown, stringNo, scanCode,
                    deadlineUs: downUs + 60_000, requiredPage: targetPage, requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                    resource: GameActionResource.StringKeys, noteNumber: target.NoteNumber));
                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(upUs, GuitarActionType.StringUp, stringNo, scanCode,
                    deadlineUs: upUs + controlGraceUs, requiredPage: targetPage, requiredSlot: targetSlot,
                    resource: GameActionResource.StringKeys));
                lastStringUp[stringNo] = upUs;
                lastStringDown[stringNo] = downUs;
                lastAttackFinishedUs = Math.Max(lastAttackFinishedUs, upUs);
            }
        }

        actions.Sort((a, b) =>
        {
            int compare = a.TimeUs.CompareTo(b.TimeUs);
            return compare != 0 ? compare : GetActionPriority(a.Type).CompareTo(GetActionPriority(b.Type));
        });
        return actions;
    }

    private static byte FindSafeChordString(ChordId chord, byte noteNumber, IReadOnlySet<byte> occupied)
    {
        byte exact = GameChordGroundTruth.FindExactCleanString(chord, noteNumber, occupied);
        return exact != 0 ? exact : GameChordGroundTruth.FindPitchClassCompatibleString(chord, noteNumber, occupied);
    }

    private static List<RawMidiNote> DeduplicateChordSourceNotes(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> sourceNotes)
    {
        const long simultaneousToleranceUs = 22_000;
        var result = new List<RawMidiNote>(sourceNotes.Count);
        var latestByPitch = new Dictionary<byte, (long AnchorUs, int Index)>();
        foreach (var note in sourceNotes.OrderBy(note => note.StartUs))
        {
            if (latestByPitch.TryGetValue(note.NoteNumber, out var previous) &&
                note.StartUs - previous.AnchorUs < simultaneousToleranceUs)
            {
                var existing = result[previous.Index];
                if (note.Velocity > existing.Velocity ||
                    (note.Velocity == existing.Velocity && note.DurationUs > existing.DurationUs))
                    result[previous.Index] = note;
                continue;
            }
            latestByPitch[note.NoteNumber] = (note.StartUs, result.Count);
            result.Add(note);
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static MouseButton ResolveChordStrumButton(
        StrumDirectionHint direction, AutoStrumMode mode, WheelPreset preset, ref bool alternate)
    {
        if (mode == AutoStrumMode.AllDown) return preset.DownStrumButton;
        if (mode == AutoStrumMode.Alternate)
        {
            var button = alternate ? preset.UpStrumButton : preset.DownStrumButton;
            alternate = !alternate;
            return button;
        }
        if (mode == AutoStrumMode.FollowMidi)
            return direction == StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow ? preset.UpStrumButton : preset.DownStrumButton;
        if (direction == StrumDirectionHint.HighToLow) return preset.UpStrumButton;
        if (direction == StrumDirectionHint.LowToHigh) return preset.DownStrumButton;
        var next = alternate ? preset.UpStrumButton : preset.DownStrumButton;
        alternate = !alternate;
        return next;
    }
    private static void CompilePluckStringActions(
        IReadOnlyList<ChordPluckEvent> pluckEvents,
        WheelPreset preset,
        GuitarCompilerOptions options,
        List<GuitarAction> actions)
    {
        if (pluckEvents == null || pluckEvents.Count == 0) return;

        var stringTriggers = new Dictionary<byte, List<(long TimeUs, long DurationUs)>>();
        foreach (var ev in pluckEvents)
        {
            if (ev.StringNo < 1 || ev.StringNo > 6) continue;
            if (!stringTriggers.TryGetValue(ev.StringNo, out var list))
            {
                list = new List<(long TimeUs, long DurationUs)>();
                stringTriggers[ev.StringNo] = list;
            }
            list.Add((ev.TimeUs, ev.DurationUs));
        }

        // 收集未被跳过拨弦事件的全局不重复起音时刻
        var uniquePluckMoments = pluckEvents.Select(p => p.TimeUs).Distinct().OrderBy(t => t).ToList();

        foreach (var kvp in stringTriggers)
        {
            byte stringNo = kvp.Key;
            ushort scanCode = (preset.StringScanCodes != null && preset.StringScanCodes.Length >= stringNo)
                ? preset.StringScanCodes[stringNo - 1]
                : GameScanCodes.ForGuitarString(stringNo);

            var items = kvp.Value;
            items.Sort((a, b) => a.TimeUs.CompareTo(b.TimeUs));

            var filtered = new List<(long TimeUs, long DurationUs)>(items.Count);
            foreach (var it in items)
            {
                if (filtered.Count == 0 || (it.TimeUs - filtered[^1].TimeUs) >= options.MinRetriggerGapUs)
                {
                    filtered.Add(it);
                }
            }

            long lastUpTime = long.MinValue / 2;
            for (int i = 0; i < filtered.Count; i++)
            {
                long sourceTime = filtered[i].TimeUs;
                long downTime = Math.Max(sourceTime, lastUpTime + options.ReleaseGapUs);
                long rawDuration = filtered[i].DurationUs;
                bool isLastOnString = (i + 1 == filtered.Count);
                long nextDownTime = isLastOnString ? long.MaxValue : filtered[i + 1].TimeUs;

                int momentIdx = uniquePluckMoments.BinarySearch(sourceTime);
                bool isLastMomentInSong = (momentIdx >= 0 && momentIdx == uniquePluckMoments.Count - 1);
                long nextGlobalTime = (!isLastMomentInSong && momentIdx >= 0 && momentIdx + 1 < uniquePluckMoments.Count)
                    ? uniquePluckMoments[momentIdx + 1]
                    : long.MaxValue;

                long holdUs = CalculateDynamicHoldUs(downTime, rawDuration, isLastOnString, nextDownTime, options, nextGlobalTime);
                long upTime = downTime + holdUs;

                if (!isLastOnString)
                {
                    long maxAllowedUp = nextDownTime - options.ReleaseGapUs;
                    if (upTime > maxAllowedUp)
                    {
                        upTime = maxAllowedUp;
                        if (upTime - downTime < options.MinHoldUs)
                        {
                            upTime = downTime + options.MinHoldUs;
                        }
                    }
                }

                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(downTime, GuitarActionType.StringDown, stringNo, scanCode));
                actions.Add(new GuitarAction(upTime, GuitarActionType.StringUp, stringNo, scanCode));
                lastUpTime = upTime;
            }
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 动态计算琴弦按键保持时长:
    /// 1. 最后一个音符且乐谱明确有延音 -> 保持饱满延音 (最高可达 MaxSustainUs)
    /// 2. 与下一个同弦或全局音符太近 (< CloseNotesThresholdUs 280ms) -> 严禁延音，立即使用点触 StringTapDurationUs 释放，留足间隔防吞音
    /// 3. 与下一个音符间隔充分 (>= 280ms) 且乐谱有延音 -> 保持延音，同时保证在下一个音符前至少留出 ReleaseGapUs
    /// </summary>
    public static long CalculateDynamicHoldUs(
        long downTime,
        long rawDurationUs,
        bool isLastOnString,
        long nextDownTime,
        GuitarCompilerOptions options,
        long nextGlobalTime = long.MaxValue)
    {
        NormalizeMinimumTiming(options);
        if (!options.EnableSustain || rawDurationUs <= options.StringTapDurationUs)
        {
            return options.StringTapDurationUs;
        }

        // 核心规则: 若整曲下一个音符在 280ms 内到来 (密集旋律/琶音进行)，严禁长按延音，必须极速点按释放
        if (nextGlobalTime != long.MaxValue && (nextGlobalTime - downTime) < options.CloseNotesThresholdUs)
        {
            return options.StringTapDurationUs;
        }

        if (isLastOnString)
        {
            // 规则 1: 最后一个音符且乐谱里明确他有延音，保持延音
            return Math.Clamp(rawDurationUs, options.StringTapDurationUs, options.MaxSustainUs);
        }

        long gapToNextDown = nextDownTime - downTime;
        if (gapToNextDown < options.CloseNotesThresholdUs)
        {
            // 规则 2: 同弦两音离得很近 (<280ms)，严禁长按，采用快速点触释放，避免连按碰撞吞音
            return options.StringTapDurationUs;
        }

        // 规则 3: 间隔充分且乐谱明确有延音，保持延音，并留足 ReleaseGap
        long maxHold = gapToNextDown - Math.Max(25_000, options.ReleaseGapUs);

        if (maxHold >= options.StringTapDurationUs)
        {
            return Math.Clamp(Math.Min(rawDurationUs, maxHold), options.StringTapDurationUs, options.MaxSustainUs);
        }

        return options.StringTapDurationUs;
    }

    private static void NormalizeMinimumTiming(GuitarCompilerOptions options)
    {
        options.MinHoldUs = Math.Max(20_000, options.MinHoldUs);
        options.StringTapDurationUs = Math.Max(options.MinHoldUs, options.StringTapDurationUs);
        options.PageKeyHoldUs = Math.Max(25_000, options.PageKeyHoldUs);
        options.ReleaseGapUs = Math.Max(22_000, options.ReleaseGapUs);
        options.MinRetriggerGapUs = Math.Max(25_000, options.MinRetriggerGapUs);
    }

    private static int GetActionPriority(GuitarActionType type) => type switch
    {
        GuitarActionType.StringUp => 0,
        GuitarActionType.PageNavUp => 0,
        GuitarActionType.PageNav => 1,
        GuitarActionType.WheelSelect => 2,
        GuitarActionType.StrumClick => 3,
        GuitarActionType.StringDown => 4,
        _ => 5
    };
}
```

## `Guitar/HybridGuitarCompiler.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;
using MidiAutoPlayer.Midi;
using MidiAutoPlayer.Recognition;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar
{
    public enum GuitarPlayModeType : byte
    {
        Fret = 0,  // 品格模式 (P1~P5: 1~24品 + P5空弦)
        Chord = 1  // 和弦模式 (P1~P4: 24个预置和弦)
    }

    public sealed class HybridCompilerOptions
    {
        /// <summary>起始模式（默认品格模式 P1）</summary>
        public GuitarPlayModeType InitialMode { get; set; } = GuitarPlayModeType.Fret;

        /// <summary>起始页码（默认 P1）</summary>
        public byte InitialPage { get; set; } = 1;
        public ChordRecognitionMode ChordMode { get; set; } = ChordRecognitionMode.Loose;
        public GuitarPlayStyle PlayStyle { get; set; } = GuitarPlayStyle.Hybrid;
        public AutoStrumMode AutoStrum { get; set; } = AutoStrumMode.Auto;

        /// <summary>模式切换键 ScanCode (Z 键 = 0x2C)</summary>
        public ushort ModeSwitchScanCode { get; set; } = 0x2C;

        /// <summary>上一页 ScanCode (R 键 = 0x13)</summary>
        public ushort PagePrevScanCode { get; set; } = 0x13;

        /// <summary>下一页 ScanCode (T 键 = 0x14)</summary>
        public ushort PageNextScanCode { get; set; } = 0x14;

        /// <summary>游戏硬件要求的最小按键按压保持时长：20ms (20,000us)</summary>
        public long MinKeyHoldUs { get; set; } = 20_000;

        /// <summary>默认点按琴弦脉冲时长：25ms (>= 20ms)</summary>
        public long StringTapDurationUs { get; set; } = 25_000;

        /// <summary>同键连按最小释放间隔：8ms</summary>
        public long ReleaseGapUs { get; set; } = 8_000;

        /// <summary>两次控制键（Z / R / T）之间的最小释放间隔：15ms</summary>
        public long ControlInterTapGapUs { get; set; } = 15_000;

        /// <summary>鼠标点击轮盘选槽后的最小稳定时间：55ms</summary>
        public long WheelMouseSettleUs { get; set; } = 55_000;

        /// <summary>鼠标左键 = 上扫音 (依实机截图设定)</summary>
        public MouseButton UpStrumMouseButton { get; set; } = MouseButton.Left;

        /// <summary>鼠标右键 = 下扫音 (依实机截图设定)</summary>
        public MouseButton DownStrumMouseButton { get; set; } = MouseButton.Right;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 混合演奏 DP 状态节点：同时表征品格或和弦状态
    /// </summary>
    public sealed record HybridStateCandidate
    {
        public GuitarPlayModeType Mode { get; init; }
        public byte Page { get; init; }           // Fret: 1..5, Chord: 1..4
        public byte Slot { get; init; }           // 1..6
        public byte Fret { get; init; }           // Fret 模式下 0..24
        public ChordId Chord { get; init; }       // Chord 模式下生效的和弦
        public ChordArticulation Articulation { get; init; }
        public StrumDirectionHint StrumDirection { get; init; }
        public IReadOnlyList<(byte StringNo, byte NoteNumber, long DurationUs)> StringNotes { get; init; }
            = Array.Empty<(byte, byte, long)>();
        public double LocalCost { get; init; }
        public bool IsHoldPositionFallback { get; init; }
    }

    public static class HybridGuitarCompiler
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// 修正后的品格页码换算：
        /// 1~24 品 -> P1~P4 (每页 6 品)
        /// 0 品 (空弦) -> P5 / S1 (第五页第 1 槽位，符合“从1开始、0结束”的 5 页轮盘设计)
        /// </summary>
        public static (byte Page, byte Slot) GetFretPageAndSlot(byte fret)
        {
            if (fret == 0)
            {
                return (5, 1); // P5 第五页：0 品空弦
            }

            byte page = (byte)(((fret - 1) / 6) + 1); // 1..4
            byte slot = (byte)(((fret - 1) % 6) + 1); // 1..6
            return (page, slot);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 完整弹奏模式（品格+和弦自动来回切换）总入口：
        /// 1. 自动计算满足 >=20ms 的配速（或跟随用户设定速度）
        /// 2. 识别全曲和声与自动半音移调
        /// 3. 使用全局 Viterbi 动态规划求解最佳【品格 <-> 和弦】切换路径
        /// 4. 编译生成带 Z 键切换、P1 重置、R/T 翻页、轮盘选槽、左右键上下扫弦及数字键拨弦的动作序列
        /// </summary>
        public static List<GuitarAction> CompileFullPerformance(
            IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawMidiNotes,
            SpeedControlManager speedManager,
            WheelPreset preset,
            HybridCompilerOptions? options = null,
            GuitarTuning? tuning = null)
        {
            if (rawMidiNotes == null || rawMidiNotes.Count == 0)
                return new List<GuitarAction>();

            options ??= new HybridCompilerOptions();
            tuning ??= GuitarTuning.Standard;

            var taggedNotes = TagPrimaryMelody(rawMidiNotes);
            var cleanNotes = MidiPolyphonyReducer.ReduceForGuitar(taggedNotes);
            if (cleanNotes.Count == 0)
                return new List<GuitarAction>();

            // 1. 自动变速计算（若用户未手动改速，则自动确保按键时长 >= 20ms）
            int autoSpeed = MidiSpeedOptimizer.CalculateAutoSpeedPercent(cleanNotes, new MidiSpeedOptimizerOptions
            {
                MinKeyHoldUs = options.MinKeyHoldUs,
                MinKeyReleaseGapUs = options.ReleaseGapUs,
                WheelMouseSettleUs = options.WheelMouseSettleUs
            });
            speedManager.ApplyAutoSpeedOnMidiImport(autoSpeed, resetUserOverride: false);

            var scaledNotes = MidiSpeedOptimizer.ScaleMidiNotes(
                cleanNotes,
                speedManager.TimeScaleFactor,
                options.MinKeyHoldUs);

            // 2. 运行 V2 和弦识别（获取自动半音移调与时间窗和弦序列）
            long rollWindowUs = (long)Math.Round(30_000 * speedManager.TimeScaleFactor);
            var chordTimeline = ChordAnalyzerV2.AnalyzeDetailed(
                scaledNotes,
                out int semitoneShift,
                new ChordAnalyzerV2Options { EnableAutoTranspose = true, Mode = options.ChordMode,
                    AttackClusterUs = rollWindowUs });

            var alignedNotes = AlignPlayableNotes(scaledNotes, semitoneShift, tuning);

            if (alignedNotes.Count == 0)
                return new List<GuitarAction>();

            // 3. Group short rolls across playable strings into one attack.
            var frames = BuildOnsetFrames(alignedNotes, rollWindowUs, tuning);

            // 4. 为每一帧构建【品格模式候选】与【和弦模式候选】
            var frameCandidates = new List<List<HybridStateCandidate>>(frames.Count);
            int activeChordIndex = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < frames.Count; i++)
            {
                var frame = frames[i];
                while (activeChordIndex + 1 < chordTimeline.Count &&
                       chordTimeline[activeChordIndex + 1].StartUs <= frame.StartUs + 20_000)
                    activeChordIndex++;
                var activeChordEv = activeChordIndex >= 0 ? chordTimeline[activeChordIndex] : null;
                var candidates = BuildFrameCandidates(frame, activeChordEv, tuning, options);
                frameCandidates.Add(candidates);
            }

            // 5. 全局 Viterbi 求解品格与和弦切换路径（内含 Z 键重置 P1 物理时间约束）
            var chosenPath = SolveHybridViterbiPath(frames, frameCandidates, options, tuning);

            // 6. 编译为底层硬件动作指令（保证所有按键按压 >= 20ms）
            return EmitHybridActions(frames, chosenPath, preset, options);
        }

        public static List<RawMidiNote> AlignPlayableNotes(
            IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> notes, int semitoneShift, GuitarTuning? tuning = null)
        {
            tuning ??= GuitarTuning.Standard;
            // One global octave choice preserves intervals. Per-note clamping
            // would collapse unrelated high or low melody notes together.
            var semitoneAligned = notes.Where(n => n.Channel != 9).OrderBy(n => n.StartUs)
                .Select(n => PhraseOctaveFolder.CloneWithPitch(n,
                    Math.Clamp(n.NoteNumber + semitoneShift, 0, 127)))
                .ToList();
            if (semitoneAligned.Count == 0) return semitoneAligned;
            int octaveShift = FretConverter.FindBestGlobalOctaveShift(semitoneAligned, tuning);
            return semitoneAligned
                .Where(n => n.NoteNumber + octaveShift >= tuning.MinPlayableNote &&
                            n.NoteNumber + octaveShift <= tuning.MaxPlayableNote)
                .Select(n => PhraseOctaveFolder.CloneWithPitch(n, n.NoteNumber + octaveShift))
                .ToList();
        }

        private static List<RawMidiNote> TagPrimaryMelody(IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> source)
        {
            var nonDrum = source.Where(note => note.Channel != 9).ToList();
            var trackCount = nonDrum.Select(note => note.TrackId).Distinct().Take(2).Count();
            var melody = trackCount <= 1
                ? PolyphonicMelodyExtractor.ExtractSingleTrackMelody(nonDrum)
                : AutoLeadFusionMode.Extract(nonDrum, tracks: null).Notes;
            var keys = melody.Select(note => (note.TrackId, note.Channel, note.StartUs, note.NoteNumber)).ToHashSet();
            return source.Select(note =>
            {
                var clone = PhraseOctaveFolder.CloneWithPitch(note, note.NoteNumber);
                clone.IsPrimaryMelody = note.IsPrimaryMelody || keys.Contains((note.TrackId, note.Channel, note.StartUs, note.NoteNumber));
                return clone;
            }).ToList();
        }

        private sealed record OnsetFrame(long StartUs, List<RawMidiNote> Notes, bool IsLeadActive, bool IsShielded,
            long NextLeadDeltaUs, byte NextLeadFretPage);

        private static List<OnsetFrame> BuildOnsetFrames(List<RawMidiNote> sortedNotes, long toleranceUs, GuitarTuning tuning)
        {
            var batches = new List<(long StartUs, List<RawMidiNote> Notes)>();
            int i = 0;
            while (i < sortedNotes.Count)
            {
                long start = sortedNotes[i].StartUs;
                var list = new List<RawMidiNote> { sortedNotes[i] };
                int j = i + 1;
                while (j < sortedNotes.Count && (sortedNotes[j].StartUs - start) <= toleranceUs)
                {
                    if (!AttackClusterPolicy.CanJoin(list, sortedNotes[j], start,
                            toleranceUs * 25 / 30, tuning))
                        break;

                    list.Add(sortedNotes[j]);
                    j++;
                }
                // A staggered two-note cross-track pair is usually two plucks.
                // Keep it separate unless a third attack confirms a chord roll.
                if (list.Count == 2 && list[0].TrackId != list[1].TrackId &&
                    list[1].StartUs > start && !list.Any(note => note.IsBrushChord))
                {
                    list.RemoveAt(1);
                    j--;
                }
                batches.Add((start, list));
                i = j;
            }
            if (batches.Count == 0) return new List<OnsetFrame>();

            int[] skyline = batches.Select(b => (int)b.Notes.Max(n => n.NoteNumber)).OrderBy(p => p).ToArray();
            int medianTop = skyline[skyline.Length / 2];
            var frames = new List<OnsetFrame>(batches.Count);
            long leadStart = long.MinValue / 2;
            long leadEnd = long.MinValue / 2;
            int leadPitch = 0;
            foreach (var batch in batches)
            {
                var top = batch.Notes.OrderByDescending(n => n.NoteNumber).ThenByDescending(n => n.Velocity).First();
                bool hasPrimary = batch.Notes.Any(note => note.IsPrimaryMelody);
                bool shielded = !hasPrimary && batch.Notes.Count == 1 && batch.StartUs - leadStart < 260_000
                    && batch.StartUs < leadEnd - 30_000 && leadPitch - top.NoteNumber >= 7;
                // The skyline is a hint. Requiring a real upper voice avoids
                // treating every bass arpeggio as a new melody attack.
                bool leadActive = hasPrimary || (!shielded && (top.NoteNumber >= 64 ||
                    (top.NoteNumber >= Math.Max(60, medianTop - 3) && batch.Notes.Count <= 2)));
                if (leadActive)
                {
                    var activeLead = batch.Notes.Where(note => note.IsPrimaryMelody)
                        .OrderByDescending(note => note.NoteNumber).FirstOrDefault() ?? top;
                    leadStart = batch.StartUs;
                    leadEnd = batch.StartUs + Math.Max(20_000, activeLead.DurationUs);
                    leadPitch = activeLead.NoteNumber;
                }
                frames.Add(new OnsetFrame(batch.StartUs, batch.Notes, leadActive, shielded, long.MaxValue, 1));
            }
            long nextLeadStart = long.MaxValue;
            byte nextLeadPage = 1;
            for (int reverseIndex = frames.Count - 1; reverseIndex >= 0; reverseIndex--)
            {
                frames[reverseIndex] = frames[reverseIndex] with
                {
                    NextLeadDeltaUs = nextLeadStart == long.MaxValue ? long.MaxValue : nextLeadStart - frames[reverseIndex].StartUs,
                    NextLeadFretPage = nextLeadPage
                };
                if (frames[reverseIndex].IsLeadActive) nextLeadStart = frames[reverseIndex].StartUs;
                if (frames[reverseIndex].IsLeadActive)
                {
                    RawMidiNote lead = frames[reverseIndex].Notes.Where(note => note.IsPrimaryMelody)
                        .OrderByDescending(note => note.NoteNumber).FirstOrDefault()
                        ?? frames[reverseIndex].Notes.OrderByDescending(note => note.NoteNumber).First();
                    nextLeadPage = FindLowestMovementFretPage(lead.NoteNumber, tuning);
                }
            }
            return frames;
        }

        private static byte FindLowestMovementFretPage(byte noteNumber, GuitarTuning tuning)
        {
            byte bestPage = 1;
            int bestDistance = int.MaxValue;
            for (byte stringNo = 1; stringNo <= 6; stringNo++)
            {
                int fret = noteNumber - tuning.GetOpenMidiNote(stringNo);
                if (fret is < 0 or > 24) continue;
                byte page = GetFretPageAndSlot((byte)fret).Page;
                int distance = Math.Abs(page - 1);
                if (distance < bestDistance) { bestDistance = distance; bestPage = page; }
            }
            return bestPage;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 为单个时间帧同时生成【品格候选】与【和弦候选】
        /// </summary>
        private static List<HybridStateCandidate> BuildFrameCandidates(
            OnsetFrame frame,
            ChordEvent? activeChordEv,
            GuitarTuning tuning,
            HybridCompilerOptions options)
        {
            var list = new List<HybridStateCandidate>();
            var distinctNotes = frame.Notes
                .GroupBy(n => n.NoteNumber)
                .Select(g => g.OrderByDescending(x => x.IsPrimaryMelody).ThenByDescending(x => x.Velocity).First())
                .OrderByDescending(n => n.IsPrimaryMelody).ThenByDescending(n => n.NoteNumber)
                .ToList();

            // ========= 候选分支 A：品格模式 (Fret Mode, P1~P5) =========
            // 提取最高主旋律音（若有可同品共奏的和声音符则一并加入）
            var primaryNote = distinctNotes.FirstOrDefault(note => note.IsPrimaryMelody) ?? distinctNotes[0];
            for (int s = 1; s <= 6; s++)
            {
                int fret = primaryNote.NoteNumber - tuning.GetOpenMidiNote(s);
                if (fret < 0 || fret > 24) continue;

                var (page, slot) = GetFretPageAndSlot((byte)fret);
                var assigned = new List<(byte StringNo, byte NoteNumber, long DurationUs)>
                {
                    ((byte)s, primaryNote.NoteNumber, primaryNote.DurationUs)
                };
                var usedStrings = new HashSet<int> { s };

                // 检查同帧其他音符是否恰好能在同一个品位（不同琴弦）同时弹出
                foreach (var other in distinctNotes.Skip(1))
                {
                    for (int s2 = 1; s2 <= 6; s2++)
                    {
                        if (usedStrings.Contains(s2)) continue;
                        if (tuning.GetOpenMidiNote(s2) + fret == other.NoteNumber)
                        {
                            usedStrings.Add(s2);
                            assigned.Add(((byte)s2, other.NoteNumber, other.DurationUs));
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 计算品格本地代价：如果原帧是 3~6 音的丰满和弦，但品格模式只能弹出 1 个单音，施加丢音代价以驱动切往和弦模式
                int droppedNotes = distinctNotes.Count - assigned.Count;
                double fretLocalCost = (frame.IsLeadActive ? droppedNotes * 1.8 : droppedNotes * 2.8)
                    + (fret > 12 ? (fret - 12) * 0.15 : 0.0);
                if (fret == 0) fretLocalCost += 0.2; // P5 空弦微调

                list.Add(new HybridStateCandidate
                {
                    Mode = GuitarPlayModeType.Fret,
                    Page = page,
                    Slot = slot,
                    Fret = (byte)fret,
                    Chord = ChordId.Unknown,
                    StringNotes = assigned,
                    LocalCost = fretLocalCost
                });
            }

            // ========= 候选分支 B：基于游戏真实弦音的混合和弦候选 =========
            // 只对本混合模式使用 chord.json 的真实音高与安全弦表。
            if (activeChordEv != null && GameChordGroundTruth.Table.TryGetValue(activeChordEv.Chord, out var chordInfo))
            {
                // Preserve a chord candidate that can play the current lead exactly.
                // The path solver evaluates the real cost of switching back later;
                // a round-trip time check here wrongly removes rapid chord arpeggios.
                byte exactLeadString = GameChordGroundTruth.FindExactCleanString(chordInfo.Id, primaryNote.NoteNumber);
                bool fullStrumPreservesLead = !frame.IsLeadActive && !frame.IsShielded ||
                    chordInfo.ActualMidi1To6.Contains(primaryNote.NoteNumber) ||
                    (options.AutoStrum != AutoStrumMode.Disabled && options.ChordMode != ChordRecognitionMode.Strict &&
                     chordInfo.ActualMidi1To6.Any(pitch => pitch % 12 == primaryNote.NoteNumber % 12));
                if (chordInfo.CanUseFullMouseStrum && distinctNotes.Count >= 3 && fullStrumPreservesLead &&
                    options.AutoStrum != AutoStrumMode.Disabled &&
                    options.PlayStyle is not GuitarPlayStyle.PluckOnly and not GuitarPlayStyle.BlockOnly)
                {
                    double chordBonusCost = Math.Max(0.1, 1.5 - activeChordEv.Confidence);
                    list.Add(new HybridStateCandidate
                    {
                        Mode = GuitarPlayModeType.Chord,
                        Page = chordInfo.Page,
                        Slot = chordInfo.Slot,
                        Chord = chordInfo.Id,
                        Articulation = ChordArticulation.Strum,
                        StrumDirection = activeChordEv.Direction,
                        LocalCost = chordBonusCost
                    });
                }

                if (options.PlayStyle != GuitarPlayStyle.StrumOnly)
                {
                    var safeNotes = new List<(byte StringNo, byte NoteNumber, long DurationUs)>();
                    var occupied = new HashSet<byte>();
                    foreach (var note in distinctNotes)
                    {
                        byte stringNo = GameChordGroundTruth.FindExactCleanString(chordInfo.Id, note.NoteNumber, occupied);
                        if (stringNo == 0 && !frame.IsLeadActive)
                            stringNo = GameChordGroundTruth.FindPitchClassCompatibleString(chordInfo.Id, note.NoteNumber, occupied);
                        if (stringNo == 0 || !occupied.Add(stringNo)) continue;

                        // For octave-equivalent accompaniment, emit the chord.json pitch actually produced by this string.
                        byte actualPitch = chordInfo.ActualMidi1To6[stringNo - 1];
                        safeNotes.Add((stringNo, actualPitch, note.DurationUs));
                    }

                    bool leadMatched = !frame.IsLeadActive && !frame.IsShielded ||
                        safeNotes.Any(note => note.NoteNumber == primaryNote.NoteNumber);
                    if (safeNotes.Count > 0 && leadMatched)
                    {
                        list.Add(new HybridStateCandidate
                        {
                            Mode = GuitarPlayModeType.Chord,
                            Page = chordInfo.Page,
                            Slot = chordInfo.Slot,
                            Chord = chordInfo.Id,
                            Articulation = safeNotes.Count >= 2 ? ChordArticulation.Block : ChordArticulation.Arpeggio,
                            StringNotes = safeNotes,
                            LocalCost = exactLeadString > 0 ? 0.35 : 0.75
                        });
                    }
                }
            }

            return list;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 计算两个混合状态之间的物理准备耗时与转移代价（核心：Z 键切模式自动重置到 P1）
        /// </summary>
        public static (long PrepareTimeUs, double Cost) CalculateHybridTransition(
            HybridStateCandidate prev,
            HybridStateCandidate cur,
            long deltaUs,
            HybridCompilerOptions opt)
        {
            if (cur.IsHoldPositionFallback)
                return prev.Mode == cur.Mode && prev.Page == cur.Page && prev.Slot == cur.Slot
                    ? (0, cur.LocalCost) : (0, double.PositiveInfinity);

            long singleKeyCycleUs = Math.Max(25_000, opt.MinKeyHoldUs)
                + Math.Max(15_000, opt.ControlInterTapGapUs);
            bool modeChanged = prev.Mode != cur.Mode;
            int pageSteps = modeChanged ? Math.Abs(cur.Page - 1) : Math.Abs(cur.Page - prev.Page);
            bool slotSelectionNeeded = modeChanged || pageSteps > 0 || cur.Slot != prev.Slot;

            long prepareUs = (modeChanged ? singleKeyCycleUs : 0)
                + pageSteps * singleKeyCycleUs
                + (slotSelectionNeeded ? Math.Max(55_000, opt.WheelMouseSettleUs) : 0);
            if (!slotSelectionNeeded) return (0, cur.LocalCost);

            long requiredGapUs = Math.Max(5_000, opt.MinKeyHoldUs) + 6_000 + prepareUs + 6_000;
            if (deltaUs < requiredGapUs || modeChanged && deltaUs < Math.Max(requiredGapUs, 220_000))
                return (prepareUs, double.PositiveInfinity);

            double cost = cur.LocalCost + (modeChanged ? 3.2 : 0.8)
                + pageSteps * (modeChanged ? 1.1 : 1.3);
            if (deltaUs < 300_000) cost += (300_000 - deltaUs) / 300_000.0 * 4.5;
            return (prepareUs, cost);
        }

        private sealed record PathNode(HybridStateCandidate State, double Cost, int Parent);

        private static List<HybridStateCandidate> SolveHybridViterbiPath(
            List<OnsetFrame> frames,
            List<List<HybridStateCandidate>> candidatesPerFrame,
            HybridCompilerOptions opt,
            GuitarTuning tuning)
        {
            var layers = new List<PathNode>[frames.Count];
            var initial = new List<PathNode>();
            foreach (var candidate in candidatesPerFrame[0])
            {
                int pageDiff = candidate.Mode == opt.InitialMode
                    ? Math.Abs(candidate.Page - opt.InitialPage) : Math.Abs(candidate.Page - 1);
                initial.Add(new PathNode(candidate, candidate.LocalCost + pageDiff * 0.5, -1));
            }
            var initialState = new HybridStateCandidate
            {
                Mode = opt.InitialMode, Page = opt.InitialPage, Slot = 0,
                LocalCost = 14, IsHoldPositionFallback = true
            };
            initial.Add(new PathNode(initialState, initialState.LocalCost, -1));
            layers[0] = initial;

            for (int i = 1; i < frames.Count; i++)
            {
                var next = new List<PathNode>();
                var bestByPosition = new Dictionary<(GuitarPlayModeType, byte, byte, byte, ChordId), int>();
                long deltaUs = Math.Max(1, frames[i].StartUs - frames[i - 1].StartUs);
                for (int k = 0; k < layers[i - 1].Count; k++)
                {
                    var previous = layers[i - 1][k];
                    foreach (var candidate in candidatesPerFrame[i].Append(MaterializeHoldState(frames[i], previous.State, tuning)))
                    {
                        if (frames[i].IsShielded && !candidate.IsHoldPositionFallback &&
                            (candidate.Mode != previous.State.Mode || candidate.Page != previous.State.Page ||
                             candidate.Slot != previous.State.Slot))
                            continue;
                        var (_, transitionCost) = CalculateHybridTransition(previous.State, candidate, deltaUs, opt);
                        if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(transitionCost)) continue;
                        double total = previous.Cost + transitionCost;
                        var key = (candidate.Mode, candidate.Page, candidate.Slot, candidate.Fret, candidate.Chord);
                        if (bestByPosition.TryGetValue(key, out int index))
                        {
                            if (total < next[index].Cost) next[index] = new PathNode(candidate, total, k);
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            bestByPosition[key] = next.Count;
                            next.Add(new PathNode(candidate, total, k));
                        }
                    }
                }
                layers[i] = next;
            }

            var path = new HybridStateCandidate[frames.Count];
            int bestIndex = 0;
            for (int j = 1; j < layers[^1].Count; j++)
                if (layers[^1][j].Cost < layers[^1][bestIndex].Cost) bestIndex = j;
            for (int i = frames.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
            {
                var node = layers[i][bestIndex];
                path[i] = node.State;
                bestIndex = node.Parent;
            }
            return path.ToList();
        }

        private static HybridStateCandidate MaterializeHoldState(OnsetFrame frame,
            HybridStateCandidate previous, GuitarTuning tuning)
        {
            var notes = new List<(byte StringNo, byte NoteNumber, long DurationUs)>();
            if (!frame.IsShielded && previous.Slot > 0)
            {
                IEnumerable<RawMidiNote> holdCandidates = frame.Notes.Any(note => note.IsPrimaryMelody)
                    ? frame.Notes.Where(note => note.IsPrimaryMelody).OrderByDescending(note => note.NoteNumber)
                    : frame.Notes.OrderByDescending(note => note.NoteNumber);
                foreach (var note in holdCandidates)
                {
                    if (previous.Mode == GuitarPlayModeType.Fret)
                    {
                        for (byte stringNo = 1; stringNo <= 6; stringNo++)
                            if (tuning.GetOpenMidiNote(stringNo) + previous.Fret == note.NoteNumber)
                            {
                                notes.Add((stringNo, note.NoteNumber, note.DurationUs));
                                break;
                            }
                    }
                    else if (GameChordGroundTruth.FindExactCleanString(previous.Chord, note.NoteNumber) is byte stringNo && stringNo > 0)
                    {
                        notes.Add((stringNo, note.NoteNumber, note.DurationUs));
                    }
                    if (notes.Count > 0) break;
                }
            }
            return previous with
            {
                StringNotes = notes,
                Articulation = ChordArticulation.Arpeggio,
                // Shielding only forbids disruptive controls. An empty hold state
                // still drops an audible attack, so it must not outrank a chord
```
