## `Guitar/WheelController.cs`

```csharp
using System.Drawing;
using MidiAutoPlayer.Input;
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

/// <summary>
/// 轮盘与安全点统一控制器: 品格与和弦完全共用底层 6 槽位坐标与安全点
/// </summary>
public sealed class WheelController
{
    private readonly object _lock = new();

    public WheelPreset Preset { get; set; }
    public int CurrentSlot { get; private set; } = 0; // 0 表示未选/初始状态
    public Point? InitialCursorPos { get; set; }
    public int MouseClickHoldMs { get; set; } = 25;

    public WheelController(WheelPreset? preset = null)
    {
        Preset = preset ?? new WheelPreset();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 翻页或重置时使当前槽位缓存失效，确保新页面的槽位绝对能被点击
    /// </summary>
    public void InvalidateCurrentSlot()
    {
        lock (_lock)
        {
            CurrentSlot = 0;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 选择轮盘指定槽位 (1..6)
    /// 若与当前槽位相同，则绝对消除重复点击
    /// </summary>
    public bool SelectSlot(IntPtr gameHwnd, int slotIndex)
    {
        if (slotIndex < 1 || slotIndex > 6) return false;

        lock (_lock)
        {
            if (CurrentSlot == slotIndex)
            {
                // 槽位未变，不重复点击
                return true;
            }

            var normPt = Preset.Slots[slotIndex - 1];
            Point screenPt = MouseSender.NormalizedClientToScreen(gameHwnd, normPt.X, normPt.Y);

            if (!screenPt.IsEmpty && MouseSender.SetPos(screenPt.X, screenPt.Y))
            {
                Thread.Sleep(4);
                if (!MouseSender.SendMouseClick(MouseButton.Left, Math.Max(25, MouseClickHoldMs)))
                    return false;

                CurrentSlot = slotIndex;
                return true;
            }

            return false;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 和弦模式语义切换
    /// </summary>
    public bool SelectChord(IntPtr gameHwnd, ChordId chord)
    {
        int slot = chord switch
        {
            ChordId.G => 1,
            ChordId.Am => 2,
            ChordId.Bm => 3,
            ChordId.C => 4,
            ChordId.D => 5,
            ChordId.Em => 6,
            _ => 0
        };

        if (slot > 0)
        {
            return SelectSlot(gameHwnd, slot);
        }
        return false;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 品格模式语义切换 (1..6品)
    /// </summary>
    public bool SelectFret(IntPtr gameHwnd, byte fret)
    {
        if (fret >= 1 && fret <= 6)
        {
            return SelectSlot(gameHwnd, fret);
        }
        return false;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 将鼠标平滑/直接移动到轮盘外扫弦安全点
    /// </summary>
    public bool MoveToStrumPoint(IntPtr gameHwnd)
    {
        Point screenPt = MouseSender.NormalizedClientToScreen(gameHwnd, Preset.StrumPoint.X, Preset.StrumPoint.Y);
        if (!screenPt.IsEmpty)
        {
            return MouseSender.SetPos(screenPt.X, screenPt.Y);
        }

        return false;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 在扫弦安全点发送扫弦点击 (左键/右键)
    /// </summary>
    public bool SendStrumClick(IntPtr gameHwnd, MouseButton button)
    {
        if (!MoveToStrumPoint(gameHwnd)) return false;
        Thread.Sleep(4);
        return MouseSender.SendMouseClick(button, Math.Max(25, MouseClickHoldMs));
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 重置控制器状态 (停止播放时调用)
    /// </summary>
    public void Reset(Point? restorePos = null)
    {
        lock (_lock)
        {
            CurrentSlot = 0;
            if (restorePos.HasValue && !restorePos.Value.IsEmpty)
            {
                MouseSender.SetPos(restorePos.Value.X, restorePos.Value.Y);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

## `Guitar/GameScanCodes.cs`

```csharp
namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar;

/// <summary>
/// 游戏键盘按键 ScanCode 映射: 游戏键 1 对应最低音弦，键 6 对应最高音弦。
/// 内部物理吉他弦号仍按标准编号: 1 弦最高音 E4，6 弦最低音 E2。
/// </summary>
public static class GameScanCodes
{
    public const ushort Digit1 = 0x02; // 游戏键 1 -> 最低音 E2 / 现实 6 弦
    public const ushort Digit2 = 0x03; // 游戏键 2 -> A2 / 现实 5 弦
    public const ushort Digit3 = 0x04; // 游戏键 3 -> D3 / 现实 4 弦
    public const ushort Digit4 = 0x05; // 游戏键 4 -> G3 / 现实 3 弦
    public const ushort Digit5 = 0x06; // 游戏键 5 -> B3 / 现实 2 弦
    public const ushort Digit6 = 0x07; // 游戏键 6 -> 最高音 E4 / 现实 1 弦

    public const ushort KeyR = 0x13;   // 上一页 (ScanCode 0x13)
    public const ushort KeyT = 0x14;   // 下一页 (ScanCode 0x14)
    public const ushort KeyZ = 0x2C;   // 吉他模式切换

    /// <summary>
    /// 将现实吉他琴弦编号 (1..6, 1=最高音E4, 6=最低音E2) 转换为游戏物理按键 ScanCode
    /// 规则：游戏键 1 是最低音弦 (现实 6 弦, E2)，游戏键 6 是最高音弦 (现实 1 弦, E4)
    /// </summary>
    public static ushort ForGuitarString(int physicalStringNumber) => physicalStringNumber switch
    {
        1 => Digit6, // 物理 1弦 (高E 64) -> 游戏按键 6 (0x07)
        2 => Digit5, // 物理 2弦 (B3 59) -> 游戏按键 5 (0x06)
        3 => Digit4, // 物理 3弦 (G3 55) -> 游戏按键 4 (0x05)
        4 => Digit3, // 物理 4弦 (D3 50) -> 游戏按键 3 (0x04)
        5 => Digit2, // 物理 5弦 (A2 45) -> 游戏按键 2 (0x03)
        6 => Digit1, // 物理 6弦 (低E 40) -> 游戏按键 1 (0x02)
        _ => Digit1
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// 将现实琴弦编号 (1..6) 转换为游戏按键数字 (1..6)
    /// 物理 1 弦 -> 游戏键 6；物理 6 弦 -> 游戏键 1
    /// </summary>
    public static int GetGameDigit(int physicalStringNumber) =>
        (physicalStringNumber >= 1 && physicalStringNumber <= 6) ? (7 - physicalStringNumber) : 1;

    /// <summary>
    /// 修复旧版启动迁移写出的游戏键升序数组。内部数组按现实物理弦序 1(高音)..6(低音) 存储，
    /// 因此标准 ScanCode 顺序必须是游戏键 6..1。自定义非标准映射保持原样。
    /// </summary>
    public static ushort[] NormalizeLegacyStringScanCodes(ushort[]? scanCodes)
    {
        if (scanCodes == null)
        {
            return [Digit6, Digit5, Digit4, Digit3, Digit2, Digit1];
        }

        if (scanCodes.Length == 6 && scanCodes.SequenceEqual(new ushort[] { Digit1, Digit2, Digit3, Digit4, Digit5, Digit6 }))
        {
            return [Digit6, Digit5, Digit4, Digit3, Digit2, Digit1];
        }

        return scanCodes;
    }
}
```

## `Input/KeyboardSender.cs`

```csharp
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Input;

public static class KeyboardSender
{
    private const int INPUT_KEYBOARD = 1;
    private const uint KEYEVENTF_EXTENDEDKEY = 0x0001;
    private const uint KEYEVENTF_KEYUP = 0x0002;
    private const uint KEYEVENTF_SCANCODE = 0x0008;
    private const uint MAPVK_VSC_TO_VK_EX = 3;
    private const uint WM_KEYDOWN = 0x0100;
    private const uint WM_KEYUP = 0x0101;

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct INPUT
    {
        public uint type;
        public MOUSEKEYBDHARDWAREINPUT mkhi;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Explicit)]
    private struct MOUSEKEYBDHARDWAREINPUT
    {
        [FieldOffset(0)]
        public HARDWAREINPUT hi;
        [FieldOffset(0)]
        public KEYBDINPUT ki;
        [FieldOffset(0)]
        public MOUSEINPUT mi;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct KEYBDINPUT
    {
        public ushort wVk;
        public ushort wScan;
        public uint dwFlags;
        public uint time;
        public IntPtr dwExtraInfo;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct HARDWAREINPUT
    {
        public uint uMsg;
        public ushort wParamL;
        public ushort wParamH;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct MOUSEINPUT
    {
        public int dx;
        public int dy;
        public uint mouseData;
        public uint dwFlags;
        public uint time;
        public IntPtr dwExtraInfo;
    }

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern uint SendInput(uint nInputs, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPArray), In] INPUT[] pInputs, int cbSize);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
    private static extern uint MapVirtualKey(uint code, uint mapType);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern bool PostMessage(IntPtr hWnd, uint msg, IntPtr wParam, IntPtr lParam);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern IntPtr GetForegroundWindow();

    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);

    public static string GetWindowTitle(IntPtr hWnd)
    {
        if (hWnd == IntPtr.Zero) return "(None)";
        var sb = new StringBuilder(256);
        if (GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, sb.Capacity) > 0)
            return sb.ToString();
        return "(Unnamed Window)";
    }

    public static bool IsForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd)
    {
        if (hWnd == IntPtr.Zero) return false;
        return GetForegroundWindow() == hWnd;
    }

    public static bool IsForeground(IntPtr hWnd) => IsForegroundWindow(hWnd);

    public static void SendBatch(IReadOnlyList<KeyAction> actions)
    {
        TrySendBatch(actions);
    }

    public static bool TrySendBatch(IReadOnlyList<KeyAction> actions)
    {
        if (actions == null || actions.Count == 0) return true;

        var inputs = new INPUT[actions.Count];
        int structSize = Marshal.SizeOf<INPUT>();

        for (int i = 0; i < actions.Count; i++)
        {
            var act = actions[i];
            uint flags = KEYEVENTF_SCANCODE;
            if (!act.IsDown) flags |= KEYEVENTF_KEYUP;
            if (act.Extended) flags |= KEYEVENTF_EXTENDEDKEY;

            inputs[i] = new INPUT
            {
                type = INPUT_KEYBOARD,
                mkhi = new MOUSEKEYBDHARDWAREINPUT
                {
                    ki = new KEYBDINPUT
                    {
                        wVk = 0,
                        wScan = act.ScanCode,
                        dwFlags = flags,
                        time = 0,
                        dwExtraInfo = IntPtr.Zero
                    }
                }
            };
        }

        return SendInput((uint)inputs.Length, inputs, structSize) == (uint)inputs.Length;
    }

    public static bool TryPostBatch(IntPtr hWnd, IReadOnlyList<KeyAction> actions)
    {
        if (hWnd == IntPtr.Zero) return false;
        if (actions == null || actions.Count == 0) return true;

        foreach (KeyAction action in actions)
        {
            uint scanCode = action.ScanCode | (action.Extended ? 0xE000u : 0u);
            uint virtualKey = MapVirtualKey(scanCode, MAPVK_VSC_TO_VK_EX);
            if (virtualKey == 0) return false;

            uint lParam = 1u | ((uint)action.ScanCode << 16);
            if (action.Extended) lParam |= 1u << 24;
            if (!action.IsDown) lParam |= (1u << 30) | (1u << 31);

            uint message = action.IsDown ? WM_KEYDOWN : WM_KEYUP;
            if (!PostMessage(hWnd, message, (IntPtr)virtualKey, (IntPtr)(long)lParam))
                return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    public static void ReleaseAllKeys(KeyPreset? preset = null)
    {
        var keysToRelease = new HashSet<(ushort ScanCode, bool Extended)>();

        if (preset != null)
        {
            foreach (var mapping in preset.Keys)
            {
                keysToRelease.Add((mapping.ScanCode, mapping.Extended));
            }
        }
        else
        {
            foreach (var item in PresetManager.DefaultMappingData)
            {
                keysToRelease.Add((item.ScanCode, item.Ext));
            }
        }

        var actions = new List<KeyAction>(keysToRelease.Count);
        foreach (var (scanCode, extended) in keysToRelease)
        {
            actions.Add(new KeyAction(0, scanCode, isDown: false, extended: extended));
        }

        SendBatch(actions);
    }
}

```

## `Input/MouseSender.cs`

```csharp
using System.Drawing;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Input;

public static class MouseSender
{
    private const int INPUT_MOUSE = 0;
    private const uint MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE = 0x0001;
    private const uint MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN = 0x0002;
    private const uint MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP = 0x0004;
    private const uint MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN = 0x0008;
    private const uint MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP = 0x0010;
    private const uint MOUSEEVENTF_VIRTUALDESK = 0x4000;
    private const uint MOUSEEVENTF_ABSOLUTE = 0x8000;

    public const int SM_XVIRTUALSCREEN = 76;
    public const int SM_YVIRTUALSCREEN = 77;
    public const int SM_CXVIRTUALSCREEN = 78;
    public const int SM_CYVIRTUALSCREEN = 79;

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    public struct POINT
    {
        public int X;
        public int Y;
        public POINT(int x, int y) { X = x; Y = y; }
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    public struct RECT
    {
        public int Left;
        public int Top;
        public int Right;
        public int Bottom;

        public int Width => Right - Left;
        public int Height => Bottom - Top;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct INPUT
    {
        public uint type;
        public MOUSEKEYBDHARDWAREINPUT mkhi;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Explicit)]
    private struct MOUSEKEYBDHARDWAREINPUT
    {
        [FieldOffset(0)]
        public MOUSEINPUT mi;
    }

    [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
    private struct MOUSEINPUT
    {
        public int dx;
        public int dy;
        public uint mouseData;
        public uint dwFlags;
        public uint time;
        public IntPtr dwExtraInfo;
    }

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern bool SetCursorPos(int x, int y);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern bool GetCursorPos(out POINT lpPoint);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern bool ClientToScreen(IntPtr hWnd, ref POINT lpPoint);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern bool ScreenToClient(IntPtr hWnd, ref POINT lpPoint);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern bool GetClientRect(IntPtr hWnd, out RECT lpRect);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern bool GetWindowRect(IntPtr hWnd, out RECT lpRect);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    public static extern int GetSystemMetrics(int nIndex);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern uint SendInput(uint nInputs, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPArray), In] INPUT[] pInputs, int cbSize);

    public static Point GetCurrentCursorPos()
    {
        GetCursorPos(out var pt);
        return new Point(pt.X, pt.Y);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 读取窗口在屏幕上的客户区原点、尺寸以及外边框，支持负坐标副屏
    /// </summary>
    public static bool GetWindowScreenInfo(IntPtr hWnd, out Point clientOrigin, out Size clientSize, out Rectangle windowRect)
    {
        clientOrigin = Point.Empty;
        clientSize = Size.Empty;
        windowRect = Rectangle.Empty;

        if (hWnd == IntPtr.Zero) return false;

        if (GetClientRect(hWnd, out var cr))
        {
            clientSize = new Size(cr.Width, cr.Height);
        }

        var pt = new POINT(0, 0);
        if (ClientToScreen(hWnd, ref pt))
        {
            clientOrigin = new Point(pt.X, pt.Y);
        }

        if (GetWindowRect(hWnd, out var wr))
        {
            windowRect = new Rectangle(wr.Left, wr.Top, wr.Width, wr.Height);
        }

        return clientSize.Width > 0 && clientSize.Height > 0;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 移动鼠标到指定屏幕物理坐标 (支持主屏、副屏、负坐标扩展屏以及 DirectInput/RawInput 游戏)
    /// </summary>
    public static bool SetPos(int screenX, int screenY)
    {
        bool cursorMoved = SetCursorPos(screenX, screenY);

        // 同步发送绝对虚拟桌面移动事件，确保跨屏 (多显示器/负坐标) 以及 DirectInput/RawInput 游戏窗口完美响应
        int vLeft = GetSystemMetrics(SM_XVIRTUALSCREEN);
        int vTop = GetSystemMetrics(SM_YVIRTUALSCREEN);
        int vWidth = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CXVIRTUALSCREEN);
        int vHeight = GetSystemMetrics(SM_CYVIRTUALSCREEN);

        bool inputMoved = false;
        if (vWidth > 1 && vHeight > 1)
        {
            int normX = (int)Math.Round((screenX - vLeft) * 65535.0 / (vWidth - 1));
            int normY = (int)Math.Round((screenY - vTop) * 65535.0 / (vHeight - 1));

            var inputs = new INPUT[1];
            inputs[0] = new INPUT
            {
                type = INPUT_MOUSE,
                mkhi = new MOUSEKEYBDHARDWAREINPUT
                {
                    mi = new MOUSEINPUT
                    {
                        dx = normX,
                        dy = normY,
                        mouseData = 0,
                        dwFlags = MOUSEEVENTF_MOVE | MOUSEEVENTF_ABSOLUTE | MOUSEEVENTF_VIRTUALDESK,
                        time = 0,
                        dwExtraInfo = IntPtr.Zero
                    }
                }
            };
            inputMoved = SendInput(1, inputs, Marshal.SizeOf<INPUT>()) == 1;
        }

        return cursorMoved && inputMoved;
    }

    public static bool SendMouseClick(MouseButton button, int holdMs = 25)
    {
        bool downSent = SendButtonAction(button, isDown: true);
        Thread.Sleep(Math.Clamp(holdMs, 5, 80));
        bool upSent = SendButtonAction(button, isDown: false);
        return downSent && upSent;
    }

    public static bool SendButtonAction(MouseButton button, bool isDown)
    {
        uint flag = button switch
        {
            MouseButton.Right => isDown ? MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN : MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP,
            _ => isDown ? MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN : MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP
        };

        var inputs = new INPUT[1];
        inputs[0] = new INPUT
        {
            type = INPUT_MOUSE,
            mkhi = new MOUSEKEYBDHARDWAREINPUT
            {
                mi = new MOUSEINPUT
                {
                    dx = 0,
                    dy = 0,
                    mouseData = 0,
                    dwFlags = flag,
                    time = 0,
                    dwExtraInfo = IntPtr.Zero
                }
            }
        };

        return SendInput(1, inputs, Marshal.SizeOf<INPUT>()) == 1;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 将归一化客户区相对坐标 (nx, ny) 转换为屏幕物理坐标 (支持主屏、副屏、负坐标扩展屏)
    /// </summary>
    public static Point NormalizedClientToScreen(IntPtr hWnd, double nx, double ny)
    {
        if (hWnd == IntPtr.Zero) return Point.Empty;

        if (!GetClientRect(hWnd, out var rect) || rect.Width <= 0 || rect.Height <= 0)
            return Point.Empty;

        var origin = new POINT(0, 0);
        if (!ClientToScreen(hWnd, ref origin))
            return Point.Empty;

        int screenX = origin.X + (int)Math.Round(nx * rect.Width);
        int screenY = origin.Y + (int)Math.Round(ny * rect.Height);

        return new Point(screenX, screenY);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// 将屏幕物理坐标转换为归一化客户区相对坐标 (nx, ny)
    /// 若 clamp=false 且点击点在窗口外部，则安全返回 null，防止异常 0.0000 录入
    /// </summary>
    public static (double nx, double ny)? ScreenToNormalizedClient(IntPtr hWnd, Point screenPoint, bool clamp = false)
    {
        if (hWnd == IntPtr.Zero) return null;

        if (!GetClientRect(hWnd, out var rect) || rect.Width <= 0 || rect.Height <= 0)
            return null;

        var origin = new POINT(0, 0);
        if (!ClientToScreen(hWnd, ref origin))
            return null;

        int clientX = screenPoint.X - origin.X;
        int clientY = screenPoint.Y - origin.Y;

        if (!clamp)
        {
            if (clientX < 0 || clientX >= rect.Width || clientY < 0 || clientY >= rect.Height)
                return null;
        }

        double nx = Math.Clamp(clientX / (double)rect.Width, 0.0, 1.0);
        double ny = Math.Clamp(clientY / (double)rect.Height, 0.0, 1.0);
        return (nx, ny);
    }
}
```

## `Midi/PlaybackPitchProjection.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Midi;

/// <summary>
/// Converts the pitch-bearing events produced by each playback compiler into MIDI
/// notes for audition, so the preview follows the same pitch decisions as playback.
/// </summary>
public static class PlaybackPitchProjection
{
    public static List<RawMidiNote> FromPianoEvents(IEnumerable<GameNoteEvent> events) =>
        events.Select(note => new RawMidiNote
        {
            StartUs = note.TimeUs,
            DurationUs = note.DurationUs,
            NoteNumber = checked((byte)(MidiConverter.GameMinMidi + note.NoteIndex)),
            Velocity = 100,
            Channel = 0
        }).ToList();

    public static List<RawMidiNote> FromFretEvents(IEnumerable<FretPlayEvent> events) =>
        events.Where(note => !note.IsSkipped)
            .Select(note => new RawMidiNote
            {
                StartUs = note.TimeUs,
                DurationUs = note.DurationUs,
                NoteNumber = note.NoteNumber,
                Velocity = note.Velocity,
                Channel = 0
            })
            .ToList();

    public static List<RawMidiNote> FromChordEvents(
        IEnumerable<ChordEvent> events,
        ChordVoicingProfile voicing,
        GuitarPlayStyle playStyle)
    {
        var result = new List<RawMidiNote>();
        foreach (var chord in events)
        {
            if (chord.Chord == ChordId.Unknown) continue;
            var attacks = chord.AttackNotes ?? new List<RawMidiNote>();

            if (attacks.Any(note => note.OrigString is >= 1 and <= 6))
            {
                foreach (var note in attacks.Where(note => note.OrigString is >= 1 and <= 6))
                {
                    result.Add(new RawMidiNote
                    {
                        StartUs = note.StartUs,
                        DurationUs = note.DurationUs,
                        NoteNumber = note.NoteNumber,
                        Velocity = note.Velocity,
                        Channel = 0,
                        OrigString = note.OrigString
                    });
                }
                continue;
            }

            ChordArticulation articulation = chord.Articulation;
            if (playStyle == GuitarPlayStyle.BlockOnly) articulation = ChordArticulation.Block;
            else if (playStyle == GuitarPlayStyle.StrumOnly) articulation = ChordArticulation.Strum;
            else if (playStyle == GuitarPlayStyle.PluckOnly) articulation = ChordArticulation.Arpeggio;

            var pitches = voicing.GetVoicing(chord.Chord);
            switch (articulation)
            {
                case ChordArticulation.Strum:
                    AddVoicingAt(chord.StartUs, attacks, pitches, result);
                    break;
                case ChordArticulation.Block:
                {
                    var targetStrings = new List<byte>();
                    var occupied = new HashSet<byte>();
                    foreach (var note in attacks.OrderBy(note => note.NoteNumber))
                    {
                        byte stringNo = voicing.MatchNoteToString(note.NoteNumber, chord.Chord, occupied, out _);
                        if (stringNo is >= 1 and <= 6 && occupied.Add(stringNo)) targetStrings.Add(stringNo);
                    }
                    if (targetStrings.Count == 0) targetStrings.AddRange([1, 2, 3, 4]);
                    foreach (byte stringNo in targetStrings)
                    {
                        if (pitches[stringNo - 1] is byte pitch)
                            result.Add(CreateNote(chord.StartUs, attacks, pitch));
                    }
                    break;
                }
                default:
                {
                    var notes = attacks.Count > 0 ? attacks : chord.SourceNotes;
                    var occupied = new HashSet<byte>();
                    foreach (var note in notes.OrderBy(note => note.StartUs))
                    {
                        byte stringNo = voicing.MatchNoteToString(note.NoteNumber, chord.Chord, occupied, out _);
                        occupied.Add(stringNo);
                        if (stringNo is >= 1 and <= 6 && pitches[stringNo - 1] is byte pitch)
                            result.Add(new RawMidiNote
                            {
                                StartUs = note.StartUs,
                                DurationUs = note.DurationUs,
                                NoteNumber = pitch,
                                Velocity = note.Velocity,
                                Channel = 0
                            });
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        return result.OrderBy(note => note.StartUs).ThenBy(note => note.NoteNumber).ToList();
    }

    private static void AddVoicingAt(long startUs, IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> attacks, byte?[] pitches, List<RawMidiNote> result)
    {
        long durationUs = attacks.Count > 0 ? attacks.Max(note => note.DurationUs) : 300_000;
        byte velocity = attacks.Count > 0 ? attacks.Max(note => note.Velocity) : (byte)100;
        foreach (byte? pitch in pitches)
        {
            if (pitch.HasValue)
                result.Add(new RawMidiNote
                {
                    StartUs = startUs,
                    DurationUs = durationUs,
                    NoteNumber = pitch.Value,
                    Velocity = velocity,
                    Channel = 0
                });
        }
    }

    private static RawMidiNote CreateNote(long startUs, IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> attacks, byte pitch) => new()
    {
        StartUs = startUs,
        DurationUs = attacks.Count > 0 ? attacks.Max(note => note.DurationUs) : 300_000,
        NoteNumber = pitch,
        Velocity = attacks.Count > 0 ? attacks.Max(note => note.Velocity) : (byte)100,
        Channel = 0
    };
}
```

## `Guitar/MidiSpeedOptimizer.cs`

```csharp
using MidiAutoPlayer.Models;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;

namespace MidiAutoPlayer.Guitar
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 速度控制状态管理：支持导入自动变速与用户手动改速覆盖
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SpeedControlManager
    {
        public const int MinAllowedSpeedPercent = 25;
        public const int MaxAllowedSpeedPercent = 200;

        /// <summary>是否处于自动变速模式（用户未手动覆盖）</summary>
        public bool IsAutoSpeed { get; private set; } = true;

        /// <summary>算法根据 20ms 约束自动计算出的推荐速度百分比 (25~100)</summary>
        public int AutoRecommendedSpeedPercent { get; private set; } = 100;

        /// <summary>用户手动设定的速度百分比（若为 null 则使用自动速度）</summary>
        public int? UserOverrideSpeedPercent { get; private set; }

        /// <summary>当前实际生效的速度百分比</summary>
        public int EffectiveSpeedPercent => IsAutoSpeed
            ? AutoRecommendedSpeedPercent
            : (UserOverrideSpeedPercent ?? AutoRecommendedSpeedPercent);

        /// <summary>时间轴缩放倍率 (TimeScale > 1.0 表示放慢，TimeUs_New = TimeUs_Raw * TimeScale)</summary>
        public double TimeScaleFactor => 100.0 / Math.Clamp(EffectiveSpeedPercent, MinAllowedSpeedPercent, MaxAllowedSpeedPercent);

        /// <summary>MIDI 导入时调用：更新自动变速结果（若用户未锁死手动速度，则自动生效）</summary>
        public void ApplyAutoSpeedOnMidiImport(int autoSpeedPercent, bool resetUserOverride = true)
        {
            AutoRecommendedSpeedPercent = Math.Clamp(autoSpeedPercent, MinAllowedSpeedPercent, 100);
            if (resetUserOverride)
            {
                IsAutoSpeed = true;
                UserOverrideSpeedPercent = null;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>用户在 UI 手动拖动速度滑块时调用</summary>
        public void SetUserManualSpeed(int userSpeedPercent)
        {
            IsAutoSpeed = false;
            UserOverrideSpeedPercent = Math.Clamp(userSpeedPercent, MinAllowedSpeedPercent, MaxAllowedSpeedPercent);
        }

        /// <summary>重置回自动配速</summary>
        public void ResetToAutoSpeed()
        {
            IsAutoSpeed = true;
            UserOverrideSpeedPercent = null;
        }
    }

    public sealed class MidiSpeedOptimizerOptions
    {
        /// <summary>游戏要求的硬件最短按键按压时长：20ms (20,000us)</summary>
        public long MinKeyHoldUs { get; set; } = 20_000;

        /// <summary>同键连续触发之间的最小抬起释放间隙：8ms (8,000us)</summary>
        public long MinKeyReleaseGapUs { get; set; } = 8_000;

        /// <summary>轮盘选槽后等待稳定的最小物理时间：50ms (50,000us)</summary>
        public long WheelMouseSettleUs { get; set; } = 50_000;

        /// <summary>控制动作（翻页/切模式）按键后的最小安全间隔：8ms</summary>
        public long ControlInterTapGapUs { get; set; } = 8_000;

        /// <summary>并发音符聚类容差：18ms 内的音符视为同一次齐奏/和弦，不计入连击间隔压缩</summary>
        public long SimultaneousToleranceUs { get; set; } = 18_000;

        /// <summary>密集间隔保护分位数；默认 1.0，按全曲最紧的有效间隔配速</summary>
        public double PercentileThreshold { get; set; } = 1.0;
    }

    public static class MidiSpeedOptimizer
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// 分析原始 MIDI 音符序列，自动计算满足“所有按键时长 >= 20ms”的最佳速度百分比 (25% ~ 100%)
        /// </summary>
        public static int CalculateAutoSpeedPercent(
            IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawNotes,
            MidiSpeedOptimizerOptions? options = null)
        {
            if (rawNotes == null || rawNotes.Count == 0)
                return 100;

            options ??= new MidiSpeedOptimizerOptions();

            // 1. 过滤打击乐通道并按起音时间排序
            var notes = rawNotes
                .Where(n => n.Channel != 9)
                .OrderBy(n => n.StartUs)
                .ToList();

            if (notes.Count <= 1)
                return 100;

            // 单次按键完整周期（按下 20ms + 抬起间隙 8ms = 28ms）
            long minSingleKeyCycleUs = options.MinKeyHoldUs + options.MinKeyReleaseGapUs;

            // 2. 聚合相同时刻的并发音符簇（Onset Clusters）
            var onsetTimes = new List<long>();
            var clusterMaxPitch = new List<byte>();
            int idx = 0;
            while (idx < notes.Count)
            {
                long start = notes[idx].StartUs;
                byte maxPitch = notes[idx].NoteNumber;
                int next = idx + 1;
                while (next < notes.Count && (notes[next].StartUs - start) <= options.SimultaneousToleranceUs)
                {
                    if (notes[next].NoteNumber > maxPitch)
                        maxPitch = notes[next].NoteNumber;
                    next++;
                }
                onsetTimes.Add(start);
                clusterMaxPitch.Add(maxPitch);
                idx = next;
            }

            if (onsetTimes.Count <= 1)
                return 100;

            var requiredScaleFactors = new List<double>();

            // 3. 逐音簇评估时间拉伸需求
            for (int i = 1; i < onsetTimes.Count; i++)
            {
                long deltaUs = onsetTimes[i] - onsetTimes[i - 1];
                if (deltaUs <= options.SimultaneousToleranceUs)
                    continue;

                bool isSamePitch = (clusterMaxPitch[i] == clusterMaxPitch[i - 1]);

                // 情况 A: 同音高连击（必然占用同一根弦，必须满足 20ms 按压 + 8ms 释放）
                if (isSamePitch)
                {
                    double scaleForRetrigger = (double)minSingleKeyCycleUs / deltaUs;
                    requiredScaleFactors.Add(scaleForRetrigger);
                }
                else
                {
                    // 情况 B: 异音连奏。若音高跨度较大（超过 4 个半音，高概率需要切槽或翻页），
                    // 前一个音必须至少按满 20ms，且留出选槽稳定时间的一半缓冲
                    int pitchDiff = Math.Abs(clusterMaxPitch[i] - clusterMaxPitch[i - 1]);
                    long requiredGapUs = options.MinKeyHoldUs + 5_000; // 基础：前音按满 20ms + 5ms 屏障缓冲

                    if (pitchDiff >= 6)
                    {
                        // 跨把位大跳，需预留单次翻页(20ms+8ms)或轮盘选槽准备时间
                        requiredGapUs += options.MinKeyHoldUs + options.ControlInterTapGapUs;
                    }

                    double scaleForTransition = (double)requiredGapUs / deltaUs;
                    requiredScaleFactors.Add(scaleForTransition);
                }
            }

            // 4. 检查单个音符本身的原始 MIDI 时值是否普遍过短
            foreach (var n in notes)
            {
                if (n.DurationUs > 2_000 && n.DurationUs < options.MinKeyHoldUs)
                {
                    // 若 MIDI 自身音符极短，记录适度拉伸系数（上限 1.5 倍，因编译器可自动延长短音）
                    double scaleForDuration = Math.Min(1.5, (double)options.MinKeyHoldUs / n.DurationUs);
                    requiredScaleFactors.Add(scaleForDuration);
                }
            }

            if (requiredScaleFactors.Count == 0)
                return 100;

            // 5. Use the tightest analyzed interval by default. A P95 speed can
            // leave short dense passages physically unreachable and cause the
            // scheduler to fall behind even when most of the song is playable.
            requiredScaleFactors.Sort();
            int pIndex = (int)Math.Clamp(
                Math.Floor(requiredScaleFactors.Count * options.PercentileThreshold),
                0,
                requiredScaleFactors.Count - 1);

            double targetScale = requiredScaleFactors[pIndex];

            // 若曲速本就从容（targetScale <= 1.0），保持 100% 原速，绝不主动加速
            if (targetScale <= 1.0)
                return 100;

            int recommendedPercent = (int)Math.Floor(100.0 / targetScale);
            return Math.Clamp(recommendedPercent, SpeedControlManager.MinAllowedSpeedPercent, 100);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// 将生效的速度百分比应用到原始 MIDI 音符列表，生成变速后的音符副本
        /// </summary>
        public static List<RawMidiNote> ScaleMidiNotes(
            IReadOnlyList<RawMidiNote> rawNotes,
            double timeScaleFactor,
            long minHoldUs = 20_000)
        {
            if (rawNotes == null || rawNotes.Count == 0)
                return new List<RawMidiNote>();

            if (Math.Abs(timeScaleFactor - 1.0) < 1e-6)
            {
                // 即使 1.0 倍速，也确保基础 DurationUs 至少为 20ms
                return rawNotes.Select(n => Copy(n, n.StartUs, Math.Max(minHoldUs, n.DurationUs))).ToList();
            }

            var scaled = new List<RawMidiNote>(rawNotes.Count);
            foreach (var n in rawNotes)
            {
                long newStartUs = (long)Math.Round(n.StartUs * timeScaleFactor);
                long newDurUs = Math.Max(minHoldUs, (long)Math.Round(n.DurationUs * timeScaleFactor));
                scaled.Add(Copy(n, newStartUs, newDurUs));
            }
            return scaled;
        }
        private static RawMidiNote Copy(RawMidiNote n, long startUs, long durationUs) => new()
        {
            StartUs = startUs, DurationUs = durationUs, NoteNumber = n.NoteNumber,
            Velocity = n.Velocity, Channel = n.Channel, TrackId = n.TrackId, Program = n.Program,
            IsPrimaryMelody = n.IsPrimaryMelody,
            OrigString = n.OrigString, OrigFret = n.OrigFret, VoiceIndex = n.VoiceIndex,
            IsLetRing = n.IsLetRing, IsPalmMute = n.IsPalmMute, IsBrushChord = n.IsBrushChord,
            IsBrushUp = n.IsBrushUp, ExplicitChordName = n.ExplicitChordName, SourceFormat = n.SourceFormat
        };
    }
}
```
